// Synchronous SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4) that runs unchanged in Node and browsers.
// WebCrypto is async-only and node:crypto is Node-only, so the core carries its own.

const K = new Uint32Array([
  0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
  0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3, 0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
  0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
  0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
  0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13, 0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
  0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
  0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
  0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208, 0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
]);

const encoder = new TextEncoder();

export function sha256Bytes(input: string | Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
  const msg = typeof input === 'string' ? encoder.encode(input) : input;
  const bitLen = msg.length * 8;
  const padded = new Uint8Array(((msg.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6);
  padded.set(msg);
  padded[msg.length] = 0x80;
  const view = new DataView(padded.buffer);
  view.setUint32(padded.length - 8, Math.floor(bitLen / 0x100000000), false);
  view.setUint32(padded.length - 4, bitLen >>> 0, false);

  const h = new Uint32Array([
    0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a, 0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19,
  ]);
  const w = new Uint32Array(64);

  for (let off = 0; off < padded.length; off += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4, false);
    for (let i = 16; i < 64; i++) {
      const a = w[i - 15], b = w[i - 2];
      const s0 = ((a >>> 7) | (a << 25)) ^ ((a >>> 18) | (a << 14)) ^ (a >>> 3);
      const s1 = ((b >>> 17) | (b << 15)) ^ ((b >>> 19) | (b << 13)) ^ (b >>> 10);
      w[i] = (w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1) >>> 0;
    }
    let a = h[0], b = h[1], c = h[2], d = h[3], e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      const S1 = ((e >>> 6) | (e << 26)) ^ ((e >>> 11) | (e << 21)) ^ ((e >>> 25) | (e << 7));
      const ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
      const t1 = (hh + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      const S0 = ((a >>> 2) | (a << 30)) ^ ((a >>> 13) | (a << 19)) ^ ((a >>> 22) | (a << 10));
      const maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
      const t2 = (S0 + maj) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0;
      d = c; c = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    h[0] = (h[0] + a) >>> 0; h[1] = (h[1] + b) >>> 0; h[2] = (h[2] + c) >>> 0; h[3] = (h[3] + d) >>> 0;
    h[4] = (h[4] + e) >>> 0; h[5] = (h[5] + f) >>> 0; h[6] = (h[6] + g) >>> 0; h[7] = (h[7] + hh) >>> 0;
  }
  const out = new Uint8Array(32);
  const ov = new DataView(out.buffer);
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) ov.setUint32(i * 4, h[i], false);
  return out;
}

export function sha256Hex(input: string | Uint8Array): string {
  const b = sha256Bytes(input);
  let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += b[i].toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  return s;
}
recovered · src/core/turbostream.ts · 3.3 KB
// Decoder for React Router "turbo-stream" payloads, the format chatgpt.com share pages embed as
// window.__reactRouterContext.streamController.enqueue("<json>").
//
// Encoding (reverse-engineered, see docs/research/02-source-formats.md):
//  - the payload is a JSON array; element 0 is the root value
//  - objects are {"_<keyIdx>": <valueIdx>}: keys and values are indices into the same array
//  - arrays hold value indices; arrays starting with a single-letter tag are typed values
//  - negative integers are sentinels (undefined / null / NaN / ±Infinity / -0)

const SENTINELS: Record<number, unknown> = {
  [-1]: undefined, // HOLE
  [-2]: Number.NaN,
  [-3]: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
  [-4]: Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY,
  [-5]: undefined,
  [-6]: -0,
  [-7]: null,
};

const TYPE_TAGS = new Set(['D', 'P', 'Y', 'R', 'E', 'S', 'M', 'Z', 'U', 'B']);

export function decodeTurboStream(payload: string | unknown[]): unknown {
  const arr = typeof payload === 'string' ? (JSON.parse(payload) as unknown[]) : payload;
  if (!Array.isArray(arr)) throw new Error('turbo-stream: payload is not an array');
  const cache = new Map<number, unknown>();

  const decode = (idx: unknown): unknown => {
    if (typeof idx !== 'number') return idx;
    if (idx < 0) return SENTINELS[idx];
    if (cache.has(idx)) return cache.get(idx);
    const v = arr[idx];
    if (Array.isArray(v)) {
      if (v.length > 0 && typeof v[0] === 'string' && TYPE_TAGS.has(v[0])) {
        const tagged = decodeTagged(v as [string, ...unknown[]]);
        cache.set(idx, tagged);
        return tagged;
      }
      const out: unknown[] = [];
      cache.set(idx, out);
      for (const item of v) out.push(decode(item));
      return out;
    }
    if (v !== null && typeof v === 'object') {
      const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      cache.set(idx, out);
      for (const [k, vi] of Object.entries(v as Record<string, unknown>)) {
        const key = k.startsWith('_') ? String(arr[Number(k.slice(1))]) : k;
        out[key] = decode(vi);
      }
      return out;
    }
    cache.set(idx, v);
    return v;
  };

  const decodeTagged = (v: [string, ...unknown[]]): unknown => {
    const [tag, a] = v;
    if (tag === 'D') return new Date(a as number).toISOString();
    if (tag === 'Y') return Symbol.for(String(a));
    // Promises/streams/errors are not needed for share pages; keep raw for debugging.
    return { $turboTag: tag, value: v.slice(1) };
  };

  return decode(0);
}

/** Extract every `streamController.enqueue("...")` string literal from an HTML page, in order. */
export function extractEnqueuedChunks(html: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const re = /streamController\.enqueue\(("(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*")\)/g;
  let m: RegExpExecArray | null;
  while ((m = re.exec(html)) !== null) {
    try {
      out.push(JSON.parse(m[1]) as string);
    } catch {
      /* ignore malformed chunk */
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Decode the first chunk that parses as a turbo-stream array. */
export function decodeFromHtml(html: string): unknown {
  for (const chunk of extractEnqueuedChunks(html)) {
    const trimmed = chunk.trim();
    if (!trimmed.startsWith('[')) continue;
    const line = trimmed.split('\n')[0];
    try {
      return decodeTurboStream(line);
    } catch {
      /* try next */
    }
  }
  throw new Error('No React Router turbo-stream payload found in HTML');
}

export function slugify(s: string, max = 48): string {
  const slug = s
    .normalize('NFKD')
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
    .slice(0, max)
    .replace(/-+$/g, '');
  return slug || 'conversation';
}

export function epochToIso(t: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (typeof t === 'number' && Number.isFinite(t) && t > 0) {
    return new Date(t < 1e12 ? t * 1000 : t).toISOString();
  }
  if (typeof t === 'string' && t) {
    const d = new Date(t);
    return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? undefined : d.toISOString();
  }
  return undefined;
}

const URL_RE = /\bhttps?:\/\/[^\s<>"'`)\]}]+/g;

export function extractUrls(text: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const m of text.match(URL_RE) ?? []) out.push(m.replace(/[.,;:!?]+$/, ''));
  return out;
}

export function truncate(s: string, n: number): string {
  if (s.length <= n) return s;
  return s.slice(0, Math.max(0, n - 1)).trimEnd() + '…';
}

export function firstLine(s: string): string {
  return (s.split('\n').find((l) => l.trim()) ?? '').trim();
}

/** Deterministic JSON with sorted keys, used for hashing. */
export function stableStringify(v: unknown): string {
  if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return JSON.stringify(v) ?? 'null';
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return '[' + v.map(stableStringify).join(',') + ']';
  const keys = Object.keys(v as Record<string, unknown>)
    .filter((k) => (v as Record<string, unknown>)[k] !== undefined)
    .sort();
  return '{' + keys.map((k) => JSON.stringify(k) + ':' + stableStringify((v as Record<string, unknown>)[k])).join(',') + '}';
}

export function isRecord(v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> {
  return v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
}

export function asString(v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v === 'string') return v;
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return '';
  if (typeof v === 'number' || typeof v === 'boolean') return String(v);
  return JSON.stringify(v, null, 2);
}

/** Pick a Markdown code fence that does not collide with the content. */
export function fence(content: string, lang = ''): string {
  let ticks = '```';
  while (content.includes(ticks)) ticks += '`';
  return `${ticks}${lang}\n${content.replace(/\n$/, '')}\n${ticks}`;
}

export function plural(n: number, word: string, pluralWord = word + 's'): string {
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? word : pluralWord}`;
}

export function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
}
recovered · src/core/zip.ts · 6.0 KB
// Minimal ZIP writer (STORE method, UTF-8 names). Browser- and Node-safe, no dependencies.
// Bundles are mostly text and small; compression is a non-goal for v1.

const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const t = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) {
    let c = n;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
    t[n] = c >>> 0;
  }
  return t;
})();

export function crc32(data: Uint8Array): number {
  let c = 0xffffffff;
  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) c = CRC_TABLE[(c ^ data[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);
  return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

export type FileMap = Record<string, string | Uint8Array>;

const enc = new TextEncoder();

function dosDateTime(d: Date): { time: number; date: number } {
  const year = Math.max(1980, d.getUTCFullYear());
  return {
    time: (d.getUTCHours() << 11) | (d.getUTCMinutes() << 5) | Math.floor(d.getUTCSeconds() / 2),
    date: ((year - 1980) << 9) | ((d.getUTCMonth() + 1) << 5) | d.getUTCDate(),
  };
}

export function zip(files: FileMap, mtime: Date = new Date()): Uint8Array {
  const { time, date } = dosDateTime(mtime);
  const locals: Uint8Array[] = [];
  const centrals: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let offset = 0;

  for (const name of Object.keys(files).sort()) {
    const raw = files[name];
    const data = typeof raw === 'string' ? enc.encode(raw) : raw;
    const nameBytes = enc.encode(name);
    const crc = crc32(data);

    const local = new Uint8Array(30 + nameBytes.length);
    const lv = new DataView(local.buffer);
    lv.setUint32(0, 0x04034b50, true);
    lv.setUint16(4, 20, true); // version needed
    lv.setUint16(6, 0x0800, true); // UTF-8 names
    lv.setUint16(8, 0, true); // STORE
    lv.setUint16(10, time, true);
    lv.setUint16(12, date, true);
    lv.setUint32(14, crc, true);
    lv.setUint32(18, data.length, true);
    lv.setUint32(22, data.length, true);
    lv.setUint16(26, nameBytes.length, true);
    lv.setUint16(28, 0, true);
    local.set(nameBytes, 30);
    locals.push(local, data);

    const central = new Uint8Array(46 + nameBytes.length);
    const cv = new DataView(central.buffer);
    cv.setUint32(0, 0x02014b50, true);
    cv.setUint16(4, 0x031e, true); // made by: unix, v3.0
    cv.setUint16(6, 20, true);
    cv.setUint16(8, 0x0800, true);
    cv.setUint16(10, 0, true);
    cv.setUint16(12, time, true);
    cv.setUint16(14, date, true);
    cv.setUint32(16, crc, true);
    cv.setUint32(20, data.length, true);
    cv.setUint32(24, data.length, true);
    cv.setUint16(28, nameBytes.length, true);
    cv.setUint32(38, (0o100644 << 16) >>> 0, true); // regular file, rw-r--r--
    cv.setUint32(42, offset, true);
    central.set(nameBytes, 46);
    centrals.push(central);

    offset += local.length + data.length;
  }

  const centralSize = centrals.reduce((n, c) => n + c.length, 0);
  const end = new Uint8Array(22);
  const ev = new DataView(end.buffer);
  ev.setUint32(0, 0x06054b50, true);
  ev.setUint16(8, centrals.length, true);
  ev.setUint16(10, centrals.length, true);
  ev.setUint32(12, centralSize, true);
  ev.setUint32(16, offset, true);

  const total = offset + centralSize + end.length;
  const out = new Uint8Array(total);
  let p = 0;
  for (const part of [...locals, ...centrals, end]) {
    out.set(part, p);
    p += part.length;
  }
  return out;
}

export interface ZipEntry {
  name: string;
  method: number; // 0 = STORE, 8 = DEFLATE
  size: number;
  compressed: Uint8Array; // raw member data (deflate-raw when method === 8)
}

/**
 * List members via the central directory, which is the reliable source of sizes. Account exports often use
 * data descriptors, so local headers carry zero sizes. ZIP64 archives are not supported.
 */
export function listZip(buf: Uint8Array): ZipEntry[] {
  const view = new DataView(buf.buffer, buf.byteOffset, buf.byteLength);
  const dec = new TextDecoder();
  let eocd = -1;
  for (let p = buf.length - 22; p >= Math.max(0, buf.length - 22 - 0xffff); p--) {
    if (view.getUint32(p, true) === 0x06054b50) {
      eocd = p;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (eocd < 0) throw new Error('Not a zip file (no end-of-central-directory record)');
  const count = view.getUint16(eocd + 10, true);
  let p = view.getUint32(eocd + 16, true);
  if (p === 0xffffffff) throw new Error('ZIP64 archives are not supported; unzip it and pass conversations.json instead');
  const out: ZipEntry[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    if (view.getUint32(p, true) !== 0x02014b50) throw new Error('Corrupt zip central directory');
    const method = view.getUint16(p + 10, true);
    const csize = view.getUint32(p + 20, true);
    const size = view.getUint32(p + 24, true);
    const nameLen = view.getUint16(p + 28, true);
    const extraLen = view.getUint16(p + 30, true);
    const commentLen = view.getUint16(p + 32, true);
    const localOff = view.getUint32(p + 42, true);
    const name = dec.decode(buf.subarray(p + 46, p + 46 + nameLen));
    const lNameLen = view.getUint16(localOff + 26, true);
    const lExtraLen = view.getUint16(localOff + 28, true);
    const start = localOff + 30 + lNameLen + lExtraLen;
    out.push({ name, method, size, compressed: buf.subarray(start, start + csize) });
    p += 46 + nameLen + extraLen + commentLen;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Extract members synchronously; `inflateRaw` is supplied by the host (node:zlib in Node). */
export function unzipSync(buf: Uint8Array, inflateRaw?: (d: Uint8Array) => Uint8Array, filter?: (name: string) => boolean): Record<string, Uint8Array> {
  const out: Record<string, Uint8Array> = {};
  for (const e of listZip(buf)) {
    if (e.name.endsWith('/') || (filter && !filter(e.name))) continue;
    if (e.method === 0) out[e.name] = e.compressed;
    else if (e.method === 8 && inflateRaw) out[e.name] = inflateRaw(e.compressed);
    else throw new Error(`${e.name}: unsupported compression method ${e.method}`);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Read a STORE-only zip (as produced above) back into a FileMap. Used by tests and `baton verify`. */
export function unzipStored(buf: Uint8Array): Record<string, Uint8Array> {
  return unzipSync(buf);
}

const s = (v: unknown): string | undefined => (typeof v === 'string' && v ? v : undefined);

function loadBundle(path: string): { bundle: BatonBundle; files: Record<string, string> } {
  const raw = readBundleFiles(path);
  if (!raw['bundle.json']) throw new Error(`${path}: no bundle.json`);
  const files: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(raw)) if (k.startsWith('files/')) files[k.slice(6)] = v;
  return { bundle: JSON.parse(raw['bundle.json']) as BatonBundle, files };
}

async function importAndWrite(text: string, filename: string, url: string | undefined, args: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<string> {
  const results = convert(text, filename, { url, redact: args.redact !== false, pick: s(args.pick) });
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const r of results) {
    const dir = resolve(results.length === 1 && s(args.out_dir) ? (args.out_dir as string) : join(s(args.out_dir) ?? 'handoffs', r.slug));
    writeFiles(dir, r.files);
    const b = r.bundle;
    out.push(
      `Bundle written to ${dir}`,
      `files: ${b.stats.artifacts.recovered} recovered · ${b.stats.artifacts.stale} stale · ${b.stats.artifacts.lost} lost · root ${b.integrity.root}`,
      '',
      args.include_handoff === false ? '' : truncate(String(r.files['HANDOFF.md']), 24000),
    );
  }
  return out.join('\n');
}

type Json = Record<string, unknown>;

export async function handleMessage(msg: Json): Promise<Json | undefined> {
  const id = msg.id as string | number | undefined;
  const method = msg.method as string | undefined;
  const reply = (result: unknown): Json => ({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id, result });
  const fail = (code: number, message: string): Json => ({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id, error: { code, message } });
  if (id === undefined || id === null) return undefined; // notification

  switch (method) {
    case 'initialize': {
      const requested = (msg.params as Json | undefined)?.protocolVersion as string | undefined;
      return reply({
        protocolVersion: requested && SUPPORTED.includes(requested) ? requested : SUPPORTED[0],
        capabilities: { tools: { listChanged: false } },
        serverInfo: { name: 'baton', version: VERSION },
        instructions: 'Baton moves AI work between tools. Use baton_import with a ChatGPT share link or an export/session file, then read the returned HANDOFF.md and continue the work. Respect the loss audit: lost files do not exist.',
      });
    }
    case 'ping':
      return reply({});
    case 'tools/list':
      return reply({ tools: TOOLS.map(({ run: _r, ...t }) => t) });
    case 'tools/call': {
      const params = (msg.params ?? {}) as Json;
      const tool = TOOLS.find((t) => t.name === params.name);
      if (!tool) return fail(-32602, `Unknown tool: ${String(params.name)}`);
      try {
        const text = await tool.run((params.arguments ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>);
        return reply({ content: [{ type: 'text', text }] });
      } catch (err) {
        return reply({ content: [{ type: 'text', text: `Error: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}` }], isError: true });
      }
    }
    case 'resources/list':
      return reply({ resources: [] });
    case 'prompts/list':
      return reply({ prompts: [] });
    default:
      return fail(-32601, `Method not found: ${String(method)}`);
  }
}

export async function startMcpServer(): Promise<void> {
  const rl = createInterface({ input: process.stdin, crlfDelay: Infinity });
  const write = (m: Json) => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(m) + '\n');
  for await (const line of rl) {
    if (!line.trim()) continue;
    let msg: Json;
    try {
      msg = JSON.parse(line) as Json;
    } catch {
      write({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: null, error: { code: -32700, message: 'Parse error' } });
      continue;
    }
    const res = await handleMessage(msg);
    if (res) write(res);
  }
}

// Node-only I/O: fetching a share URL, reading files/zips, locating Claude Code sessions, writing bundles.

import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { homedir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { inflateRawSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { unzipSync, zip, type FileMap } from '../core/zip.ts';
import { pickExportMember } from '../pipeline.ts';

export interface LoadedInput {
  text: string;
  filename: string;
  url?: string;
}

const SHARE_HOSTS = new Set(['chatgpt.com', 'chat.openai.com']);
const UA = 'Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/126.0 Safari/537.36 Baton/0.1';

export function isShareUrl(s: string): boolean {
  try {
    const u = new URL(s);
    return u.protocol === 'https:' && SHARE_HOSTS.has(u.hostname) && /^\/(?:share|s)\/[A-Za-z0-9-]+\/?$/.test(u.pathname);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** Fetch exactly one user-supplied public share page. No crawling, no auth, no retries beyond one. */
export async function fetchShare(url: string, timeoutMs = 30000): Promise<LoadedInput> {
  if (!isShareUrl(url)) {
    throw new Error(`Only public ChatGPT share links are fetched (https://chatgpt.com/share/<id>). For other sources, export the data and pass the file. Got: ${url}`);
  }
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'user-agent': UA, accept: 'text/html' }, signal: ctrl.signal, redirect: 'follow' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Fetching share link failed: HTTP ${res.status}. The link may be private, deleted, or rate-limited. Try saving the page (Ctrl/Cmd+S) and passing the .html file.`);
    const text = await res.text();
    if (!text.includes('streamController.enqueue')) {
      throw new Error('The page did not contain conversation data (possibly a login or bot-check page). Save the page from your browser and pass the .html file instead.');
    }
    return { text, filename: 'share.html', url };
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(t);
  }
}

/** Read a file or a ZIP export. For ZIPs, conversations.json is selected automatically. */
export function readInputFile(path: string): LoadedInput {
  const buf = readFileSync(path);
  if (buf.length >= 4 && buf.readUInt32LE(0) === 0x04034b50) {
    const members = unzipSync(new Uint8Array(buf), (d) => new Uint8Array(inflateRawSync(d)), (n) => /\.(json|jsonl|html?)$/i.test(n));
    const name = pickExportMember(Object.keys(members));
    if (!name) throw new Error(`${path}: zip contains no conversations.json / .jsonl / .html`);
    return { text: new TextDecoder().decode(members[name]), filename: name };
  }
  if (buf.length >= 2 && buf[0] === 0x1f && buf[1] === 0x8b) {
    // .gz (e.g. fixtures)
    const { gunzipSync } = require_zlib();
    return { text: gunzipSync(buf).toString('utf8'), filename: path.replace(/\.gz$/, '') };
  }
  return { text: buf.toString('utf8'), filename: path };
}

function require_zlib(): typeof import('node:zlib') {
  // node:zlib is already loaded above for inflateRawSync; re-import lazily for gunzip.
  return process.getBuiltinModule('node:zlib') as typeof import('node:zlib');
}

export async function loadInput(source: string): Promise<LoadedInput> {
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(source)) return fetchShare(source);
  if (!existsSync(source)) throw new Error(`No such file: ${source}`);
  if (statSync(source).isDirectory()) {
    const latest = newestJsonl(source);
    if (!latest) throw new Error(`${source} is a directory without .jsonl session logs`);
    return readInputFile(latest);
  }
  return readInputFile(source);
}

// ───────────────────────────── Claude Code sessions ─────────────────────────────

export function claudeProjectsDir(): string {
  return process.env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR ? join(process.env.CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, 'projects') : join(homedir(), '.claude', 'projects');
}

/** Claude Code stores sessions under ~/.claude/projects/<cwd with / and . replaced by ->/<session>.jsonl */
export function projectSlug(cwd: string): string {
  return cwd.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '-');
}

function newestJsonl(dir: string): string | undefined {
  const files = readdirSync(dir)
    .filter((f) => f.endsWith('.jsonl'))
    .map((f) => ({ f: join(dir, f), m: statSync(join(dir, f)).mtimeMs }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.m - a.m);
  return files[0]?.f;
}

export function findSession(idOrLatest = 'latest', cwd = process.cwd()): string {
  const base = claudeProjectsDir();
  const projDir = join(base, projectSlug(cwd));
  if (idOrLatest !== 'latest') {
    const direct = join(projDir, `${idOrLatest}.jsonl`);
    if (existsSync(direct)) return direct;
    for (const d of existsSync(base) ? readdirSync(base) : []) {
      const p = join(base, d, `${idOrLatest}.jsonl`);
      if (existsSync(p)) return p;
    }
    throw new Error(`Session ${idOrLatest} not found under ${base}`);
  }
  const latest = existsSync(projDir) ? newestJsonl(projDir) : undefined;
  if (!latest) throw new Error(`No Claude Code sessions found for ${cwd} (looked in ${projDir})`);
  return latest;
}

// ───────────────────────────── writing ─────────────────────────────

/** Write a file map under `outDir`, refusing any path that would escape it. Returns written paths. */
export function writeFiles(outDir: string, files: FileMap): string[] {
  const root = resolve(outDir);
  const written: string[] = [];
  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
    const target = resolve(root, rel);
    if (target !== root && !target.startsWith(root + sep)) throw new Error(`Refusing to write outside ${root}: ${rel}`);
    mkdirSync(dirname(target), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(target, content);
    written.push(relative(process.cwd(), target) || target);
  }
  return written;
}

export function writeZip(path: string, files: FileMap, prefix = ''): void {
  const prefixed: FileMap = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(files)) prefixed[prefix ? `${prefix}/${k}` : k] = v;
  mkdirSync(dirname(resolve(path)), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(path, zip(prefixed));
}

/** Read a bundle back (directory, bundle.json, or .zip) for verify/sink. */
export function readBundleFiles(path: string): Record<string, string> {
  const dec = new TextDecoder();
  if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) {
    const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    const walk = (d: string) => {
      for (const f of readdirSync(d)) {
        const p = join(d, f);
        if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) walk(p);
        else out[relative(path, p).split(sep).join('/')] = readFileSync(p, 'utf8');
      }
    };
    walk(path);
    return out;
  }
  const buf = readFileSync(path);
  if (buf.readUInt32LE(0) === 0x04034b50) {
    const members = unzipSync(new Uint8Array(buf), (d) => new Uint8Array(inflateRawSync(d)));
    const out: Record<string, string> = {};
    const names = Object.keys(members);
    const bundleJson = names.find((n) => n.endsWith('bundle.json'));
    const prefix = bundleJson ? bundleJson.slice(0, -'bundle.json'.length) : '';
    for (const n of names) if (n.startsWith(prefix)) out[n.slice(prefix.length)] = dec.decode(members[n]);
    return out;
  }
  return { 'bundle.json': buf.toString('utf8') };
}
recovered · src/node/post.ts · 1.8 KB
// Opt-in delivery of sink payloads. Credentials come only from environment variables; nothing is stored.

import type { SinkPayload } from '../sinks/index.ts';

function need(name: string): string {
  const v = process.env[name];
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Missing environment variable ${name}`);
  return v;
}

export interface PostResult {
  ok: boolean;
  status: number;
  url: string;
  response: string;
}

export async function postSink(p: SinkPayload): Promise<PostResult> {
  let url: string;
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'content-type': 'application/json', accept: 'application/json' };
  let body = p.body;
  switch (p.sink) {
    case 'salesforce':
      url = need('SALESFORCE_INSTANCE_URL').replace(/\/$/, '') + p.path;
      headers.authorization = `Bearer ${need('SALESFORCE_ACCESS_TOKEN')}`;
      break;
    case 'jira': {
      url = need('JIRA_BASE_URL').replace(/\/$/, '') + p.path;
      headers.authorization = `Basic ${btoa(`${need('JIRA_EMAIL')}:${need('JIRA_API_TOKEN')}`)}`;
      const project = process.env.JIRA_PROJECT;
      const json = JSON.stringify(body);
      if (json.includes('${JIRA_PROJECT}')) {
        if (!project) throw new Error('Set JIRA_PROJECT or pass --jira-project');
        body = JSON.parse(json.replace('${JIRA_PROJECT}', project));
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'zendesk':
      url = `https://${need('ZENDESK_SUBDOMAIN')}.zendesk.com${p.path}`;
      headers.authorization = `Basic ${btoa(`${need('ZENDESK_EMAIL')}/token:${need('ZENDESK_API_TOKEN')}`)}`;
      break;
    case 'slack':
      url = need('SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL');
      break;
  }
  const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
  return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, url: url.replace(/(hooks\.slack\.com\/services\/)\S+/, '$1…'), response: (await res.text()).slice(0, 2000) };
}
