// ChatGPT: public share pages (HTML / turbo-stream) and official data exports (conversations.json).
// Both carry the same conversation object: { title, mapping{id:{parent,children,message}}, current_node }.

import type { BatonEvent, LossItem, ParsedConversation } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { decodeFromHtml, decodeTurboStream } from '../core/turbostream.ts';
import { asString, epochToIso, isRecord } from '../core/util.ts';

type Rec = Record<string, unknown>;
type DraftEvent = Omit<BatonEvent, 'seq' | 'hash'>;

export const REDACTED_PLACEHOLDER = 'The output of this plugin was redacted.';

/** Locate the conversation object inside a decoded share-page loader payload. */
export function findShareConversation(root: unknown): Rec {
  if (isRecord(root)) {
    const loader = root.loaderData;
    if (isRecord(loader)) {
      for (const [route, data] of Object.entries(loader)) {
        if (!route.includes('share') || !isRecord(data)) continue;
        const sr = data.serverResponse;
        if (isRecord(sr) && isRecord(sr.data) && isRecord(sr.data.mapping)) return sr.data;
      }
    }
  }
  // Fallback: depth-first search for an object with mapping + current_node.
  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
  const stack: unknown[] = [root];
  while (stack.length) {
    const v = stack.pop();
    if (!isRecord(v) && !Array.isArray(v)) continue;
    if (seen.has(v)) continue;
    seen.add(v);
    if (isRecord(v) && isRecord(v.mapping) && typeof v.current_node === 'string') return v;
    for (const child of Array.isArray(v) ? v : Object.values(v)) stack.push(child);
  }
  throw new Error('ChatGPT share: conversation data not found in page payload');
}

export function parseChatGPTShareHtml(html: string, url?: string): ParsedConversation {
  const root = decodeFromHtml(html);
  const conv = findShareConversation(root);
  return parseChatGPTConversation(conv, { kind: 'chatgpt-share', url });
}

export function parseChatGPTTurboStream(payload: string, url?: string): ParsedConversation {
  const conv = findShareConversation(decodeTurboStream(payload.trim().split('\n')[0]));
  return parseChatGPTConversation(conv, { kind: 'chatgpt-share', url });
}

/** conversations.json from the account export: an array of conversation objects. */
export function parseChatGPTExport(json: unknown): ParsedConversation[] {
  const list = Array.isArray(json) ? json : isRecord(json) ? [json] : [];
  return list.filter((c) => isRecord(c) && isRecord(c.mapping)).map((c) => parseChatGPTConversation(c as Rec, { kind: 'chatgpt-export' }));
}

function activePath(mapping: Rec, currentNode: string | undefined): Rec[] {
  const nodes: Rec[] = [];
  let id = currentNode;
  const guard = new Set<string>();
  if (!id || !isRecord(mapping[id])) {
    // No current node: pick the deepest leaf following the last child at each step.
    const rootId = Object.keys(mapping).find((k) => {
      const n = mapping[k];
      return isRecord(n) && !n.parent;
    });
    id = rootId;
    while (id && isRecord(mapping[id])) {
      const kids = (mapping[id] as Rec).children;
      if (!Array.isArray(kids) || kids.length === 0) break;
      id = String(kids[kids.length - 1]);
    }
  }
  while (id && isRecord(mapping[id]) && !guard.has(id)) {
    guard.add(id);
    const node = mapping[id] as Rec;
    nodes.push(node);
    id = typeof node.parent === 'string' ? node.parent : undefined;
  }
  return nodes.reverse();
}

/** Replace ChatGPT private-use entity markers (U+E200 name U+E202 args… U+E201) with readable text. */
export function cleanEntities(text: string, counters: { cites: number; images: number }): string {
  return text
    .replace(/([a-z_]+)((?:[^]*)*)/g, (_m, name: string, argStr: string) => {
      const args = argStr.split('').filter(Boolean);
      if (name === 'cite') {
        counters.cites++;
        return ` [cite: ${args.join(', ')}]`;
      }
      if (name === 'url' && args.length >= 2 && /^https?:\/\//.test(args[1])) return `[${args[0]}](${args[1]})`;
      if (name === 'image_group') {
        counters.images++;
        try {
          const q = JSON.parse(args[0] ?? '{}') as { query?: string[] };
          return `*[image: ${(q.query ?? []).join('; ')}]*`;
        } catch {
          return '*[image]*';
        }
      }
      return `[${name}${args.length ? ': ' + args.join(', ') : ''}]`;
    })
    .replace(/[-]/g, '');
}

/** Strip GenUI JSX (when no fallbackMarkdown exists) into plain Markdown. */
export function jsxToMarkdown(text: string, counters: { cites: number; images: number }): string {
  return text
    .replace(/<Cite refs=\{\[([^\]]*)\]\}\s*\/>/g, (_m, refs: string) => {
      counters.cites++;
      return ` [cite: ${refs.replace(/["\s]/g, '').split(',').join(', ')}]`;
    })
    .replace(/<AsyncImage[^>]*query="([^"]*)"[^>]*\/>/g, (_m, q: string) => {
      counters.images++;
      return `*[image: ${q}]*`;
    })
    .replace(/<button[^>]*GenUI\.openUrl\("([^"]+)"\)[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/button>/g, (_m, url: string, label: string) => `[${label.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '').trim()}](${url})`)
    .replace(/<title[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/title\s*>?/g, '**$1**')
    .replace(/<badge[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/badge>/g, '`$1`')
    .replace(/<\/?(?:box|grid|grid-item|row|text|divider|icon|list|list-item)\b[^>]*\/?>/g, '')
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n');
}

export interface ChatGPTOptions {
  kind: 'chatgpt-share' | 'chatgpt-export';
  url?: string;
}

export function parseChatGPTConversation(conv: Rec, opts: ChatGPTOptions): ParsedConversation {
  const mapping = (conv.mapping ?? {}) as Rec;
  const path = activePath(mapping, typeof conv.current_node === 'string' ? conv.current_node : undefined);
  const counters = { cites: 0, images: 0 };
  const events: DraftEvent[] = [];
  let userAttachments = 0;
  let imageParts = 0;
  let models = new Set<string>();

  for (const node of path) {
    const msg = node.message;
    if (!isRecord(msg)) continue;
    const author = (msg.author ?? {}) as Rec;
    const role = String(author.role ?? 'assistant');
    const content = (msg.content ?? {}) as Rec;
    const ct = String(content.content_type ?? 'text');
    const md = (msg.metadata ?? {}) as Rec;
    const recipient = typeof msg.recipient === 'string' ? msg.recipient : 'all';
    const time = epochToIso(msg.create_time);
    const model = typeof md.model_slug === 'string' ? md.model_slug : typeof md.resolved_model_slug === 'string' ? md.resolved_model_slug : undefined;
    if (model) models.add(model);
    const hiddenInUi = md.is_visually_hidden_from_conversation === true;
    const titles = Array.isArray(md.reasoning_titles) ? (md.reasoning_titles as unknown[]).map(String) : [];
    const title = typeof md.reasoning_title === 'string' ? md.reasoning_title : titles[0];
    const base = { id: String(msg.id ?? node.id ?? ''), time, model };

    // Private context: system prompts, memory, custom instructions.
    if (role === 'system' || ct === 'model_editable_context' || ct === 'user_editable_context') {
      const txt = ct === 'model_editable_context' ? asString(content.model_set_context) : partsText(content);
      if (!txt.trim()) continue; // empty scaffolding; nothing to keep or to lose
      events.push({ ...base, kind: 'note', role: 'system', text: txt, hidden: true, meta: { private: true, contentType: ct } });
      continue;
    }

    if (ct === 'thoughts') {
      const thoughts = Array.isArray(content.thoughts) ? (content.thoughts as Rec[]) : [];
      const text = thoughts
        .map((t) => {
          const s = asString(t.summary).trim();
          const c = asString(t.content).trim();
          return c ? `**${s}.** ${c}` : s ? `**${s}.**` : '';
        })
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('\n\n');
      if (text) events.push({ ...base, kind: 'reasoning', role: 'assistant', text, meta: { granularity: 'summary' } });
      continue;
    }

    if (ct === 'reasoning_recap') {
      events.push({ ...base, kind: 'note', role: 'assistant', text: asString(content.content), meta: { recap: true } });
      continue;
    }

    if (role === 'tool') {
      const text = ct === 'execution_output' ? asString(content.text) : partsText(content) || asString(content.text) || asString(content.result);
      const redacted = text.trim() === REDACTED_PLACEHOLDER;
      events.push({
        ...base,
        kind: 'tool_result',
        role: 'tool',
        text: redacted ? '' : text,
        tool: { name: String(author.name ?? recipient), title },
        redactedBySource: redacted || undefined,
        hidden: hiddenInUi || undefined,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (role === 'assistant' && recipient !== 'all' && recipient !== 'assistant') {
      const input = ct === 'code' ? asString(content.text) : partsText(content);
      const redacted = input.trim() === REDACTED_PLACEHOLDER || input.trim() === '';
      events.push({
        ...base,
        kind: 'tool_call',
        role: 'assistant',
        text: title ?? '',
        tool: {
          name: recipient,
          title: titles.length > 1 ? titles.join(' · ') : title,
          language: ct === 'code' ? normaliseLang(asString(content.language), input) : undefined,
          input: redacted ? undefined : input,
        },
        redactedBySource: redacted || undefined,
        hidden: hiddenInUi || undefined,
      });
      continue;
    }

    // Plain prose (user or assistant).
    let text: string;
    const dil = isRecord(md.model_dil_v2) ? md.model_dil_v2 : undefined;
    if (role === 'assistant' && dil && typeof dil.fallbackMarkdown === 'string' && dil.fallbackMarkdown.trim()) {
      text = cleanEntities(dil.fallbackMarkdown, counters);
    } else {
      const parts = Array.isArray(content.parts) ? content.parts : [];
      text = parts
        .map((p) => {
          if (typeof p === 'string') return p;
          if (isRecord(p) && String(p.content_type ?? '').includes('image')) {
            imageParts++;
            return '*[image attachment, not included by source]*';
          }
          return asString(p);
        })
        .join('\n');
      text = role === 'assistant' ? jsxToMarkdown(cleanEntities(text, counters), counters) : text;
    }
    if (role === 'user' && Array.isArray(md.attachments)) {
      for (const a of md.attachments as Rec[]) {
        userAttachments++;
        events.push({ ...base, id: `${base.id}-att-${userAttachments}`, kind: 'attachment', role: 'user', text: `Attached file: ${asString(a.name) || 'unnamed'}`, meta: { name: a.name, mimeType: a.mime_type ?? a.mimeType, size: a.size } });
      }
    }
    if (!text.trim()) continue;
    events.push({
      ...base,
      kind: 'message',
      role: role === 'user' ? 'user' : 'assistant',
      text,
      hidden: hiddenInUi || undefined,
      meta: hiddenInUi ? { channel: 'commentary' } : undefined,
    });
  }

  const loss: LossItem[] = [];
  const offPath = Object.keys(mapping).length - path.length;
  if (offPath > 0) loss.push({ category: 'branches', count: offPath, severity: 'info', detail: `${offPath} message node(s) belong to edited/regenerated branches that are not on the active path and were not exported.` });
  if (counters.cites) loss.push({ category: 'citations', count: counters.cites, severity: 'warn', detail: `${counters.cites} inline citation marker(s) point to search results that the source did not include; only marker ids survive. See links for the source's URL list.` });
  if (counters.images + imageParts) loss.push({ category: 'images', count: counters.images + imageParts, severity: 'info', detail: 'Images shown in the conversation are not included; placeholders keep their search query where available.' });
  if (userAttachments) loss.push({ category: 'user-attachments', count: userAttachments, severity: 'warn', detail: 'Files the user uploaded are referenced by name only; their contents are not part of the source.' });
  if (events.some((e) => e.kind === 'reasoning')) loss.push({ category: 'reasoning-depth', count: events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'reasoning').length, severity: 'info', detail: 'Reasoning is available as the summaries ChatGPT displays, not the full internal chain of thought.' });

  const modelInfo = isRecord(conv.model) ? asString(conv.model.slug) : typeof conv.default_model_slug === 'string' ? conv.default_model_slug : undefined;
  const sourceLinks = Array.isArray(conv.safe_urls) ? (conv.safe_urls as unknown[]).filter((u): u is string => typeof u === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//.test(u)) : [];
  const conversationId = asString(conv.conversation_id) || asString(conv.id) || undefined;

  return {
    title: asString(conv.title) || 'Untitled ChatGPT conversation',
    source: {
      kind: opts.kind,
      tool: 'ChatGPT',
      url: opts.url,
      conversationId,
      createdAt: epochToIso(conv.create_time),
      updatedAt: epochToIso(conv.update_time),
      model: modelInfo || [...models][0],
    },
    events,
    sourceLinks,
    loss,
  };
}

function partsText(content: Rec): string {
  const parts = Array.isArray(content.parts) ? content.parts : [];
  return parts.map((p) => (typeof p === 'string' ? p : asString(p))).join('\n');
}

function normaliseLang(lang: string, code: string): string {
  if (lang && lang !== 'unknown') return lang;
  if (/^\s*(bash|sh)\s+-l?c\b/.test(code) || /^\s*(cd|ls|cat|mkdir|cp|python|pip|zip)\b/.test(code)) return 'bash';
  if (/^\s*(import|from|def|print)\b/m.test(code)) return 'python';
  return '';
}
recovered · src/parsers/claude.ts · 11.3 KB
// Claude.ai account export (conversations.json) and Claude Code session logs (*.jsonl).

import type { BatonEvent, LossItem, ParsedConversation } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { asString, epochToIso, isRecord } from '../core/util.ts';

type Rec = Record<string, unknown>;
type DraftEvent = Omit<BatonEvent, 'seq' | 'hash'>;

// ───────────────────────────── claude.ai export ─────────────────────────────

export function parseClaudeExport(json: unknown): ParsedConversation[] {
  const list = Array.isArray(json) ? json : isRecord(json) ? [json] : [];
  return list.filter((c) => isRecord(c) && Array.isArray(c.chat_messages)).map((c) => parseClaudeConversation(c as Rec));
}

function parseClaudeConversation(conv: Rec): ParsedConversation {
  const events: DraftEvent[] = [];
  const attachments: { path: string; content: string; note?: string }[] = [];
  let filesWithoutContent = 0;
  let n = 0;

  for (const m of conv.chat_messages as Rec[]) {
    const role = m.sender === 'human' ? 'user' : 'assistant';
    const time = epochToIso(m.created_at);
    const baseId = asString(m.uuid) || `m${++n}`;
    const blocks = Array.isArray(m.content) ? (m.content as Rec[]) : [];
    if (blocks.length) {
      blocks.forEach((b, i) => pushBlock(events, b, `${baseId}-${i}`, role, time));
    } else if (asString(m.text).trim()) {
      events.push({ id: baseId, kind: 'message', role, time, text: asString(m.text) });
    }
    for (const a of (Array.isArray(m.attachments) ? m.attachments : []) as Rec[]) {
      const name = asString(a.file_name) || 'attachment.txt';
      const extracted = asString(a.extracted_content);
      events.push({ id: `${baseId}-att-${name}`, kind: 'attachment', role: 'user', time, text: `Attached file: ${name}`, meta: { size: a.file_size, type: a.file_type } });
      if (extracted) attachments.push({ path: `attachments/${name}`, content: extracted, note: 'text extracted by Claude at upload time' });
      else filesWithoutContent++;
    }
    for (const f of (Array.isArray(m.files) ? m.files : []) as Rec[]) {
      filesWithoutContent++;
      events.push({ id: `${baseId}-file-${asString(f.file_name)}`, kind: 'attachment', role: 'user', time, text: `Attached file: ${asString(f.file_name) || 'file'}` });
    }
  }

  const loss: LossItem[] = [];
  if (filesWithoutContent) loss.push({ category: 'user-attachments', count: filesWithoutContent, severity: 'warn', detail: 'Uploaded files without extracted text (images, binaries) are referenced by name only.' });
  loss.push({ category: 'memory', count: 1, severity: 'info', detail: 'Claude exports do not include memory or project knowledge; only conversation messages.' });

  return {
    title: asString(conv.name) || 'Untitled Claude conversation',
    source: { kind: 'claude-export', tool: 'Claude', conversationId: asString(conv.uuid) || undefined, createdAt: epochToIso(conv.created_at), updatedAt: epochToIso(conv.updated_at) },
    events,
    attachments,
    loss,
  };
}

// ───────────────────────────── shared block handling ─────────────────────────────

function pushBlock(events: DraftEvent[], b: Rec, id: string, role: 'user' | 'assistant', time?: string, model?: string): void {
  const type = asString(b.type);
  if (type === 'text') {
    const text = asString(b.text);
    if (text.trim()) events.push({ id, kind: 'message', role, time, model, text });
  } else if (type === 'thinking' || type === 'redacted_thinking') {
    const text = asString(b.thinking);
    events.push({
      id,
      kind: 'reasoning',
      role: 'assistant',
      time,
      model,
      text,
      redactedBySource: !text.trim() || type === 'redacted_thinking' || undefined,
      meta: { granularity: 'full' },
    });
  } else if (type === 'tool_use' || type === 'server_tool_use') {
    const input = b.input;
    const name = asString(b.name) || 'tool';
    const args = FILE_TOOLS.has(name) && isRecord(input) ? input : undefined;
    events.push({ id, kind: 'tool_call', role: 'assistant', time, model, text: describeToolCall(name, input), tool: { name, callId: asString(b.id) || undefined, input: toolInputText(name, input), language: toolLang(name), args } });
  } else if (type === 'tool_result' || type.endsWith('_tool_result')) {
    const text = toolResultText(b.content);
    events.push({ id, kind: 'tool_result', role: 'tool', time, text, tool: { name: asString(b.name) || 'tool', callId: asString(b.tool_use_id) || undefined }, meta: b.is_error ? { isError: true } : undefined });
  } else if (type === 'image' || type === 'document') {
    events.push({ id, kind: 'attachment', role, time, text: `*[${type} attachment, binary content not included]*` });
  }
}

function describeToolCall(name: string, input: unknown): string {
  if (!isRecord(input)) return name;
  const hint = input.description ?? input.file_path ?? input.path ?? input.query ?? input.url ?? input.pattern ?? input.command;
  return hint ? `${name}: ${asString(hint).split('\n')[0].slice(0, 160)}` : name;
}

// Tools whose structured args drive file recovery (see recover/artifacts.ts).
const FILE_TOOLS = new Set(['Write', 'Edit', 'MultiEdit', 'NotebookEdit']);

function toolInputText(name: string, input: unknown): string {
  if (isRecord(input)) {
    if (name === 'Bash' && typeof input.command === 'string') return input.command;
    // File bodies live in tool.args and are recovered as artifacts; keep the display compact.
    if (name === 'Write' && typeof input.content === 'string') return `${asString(input.file_path)}  (${input.content.length} chars → recovered file)`;
    if (name === 'Edit') return `${asString(input.file_path)}  (edit: ${asString(input.old_string).length} → ${asString(input.new_string).length} chars)`;
  }
  return asString(input);
}

function toolLang(name: string): string | undefined {
  if (name === 'Bash') return 'bash';
  return 'json';
}

function toolResultText(content: unknown): string {
  if (typeof content === 'string') return content;
  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return asString(content);
  return (content as Rec[])
    .map((c) => {
      if (c.type === 'text') return asString(c.text);
      if (c.type === 'image') return '*[image output]*';
      if (c.type === 'tool_reference') return `(tool loaded: ${asString(c.tool_name)})`;
      return asString(c);
    })
    .join('\n');
}

// ───────────────────────────── Claude Code JSONL ─────────────────────────────

const SYSTEM_REMINDER = /<system-reminder>[\s\S]*?<\/system-reminder>/g;

export function parseClaudeCodeJsonl(text: string): ParsedConversation {
  const events: DraftEvent[] = [];
  let title = '';
  let sessionId: string | undefined;
  let cwd: string | undefined;
  let gitBranch: string | undefined;
  let model: string | undefined;
  let firstTime: string | undefined;
  let lastTime: string | undefined;
  let reminders = 0;
  let badLines = 0;
  let sidechain = 0;
  const toolNames = new Map<string, string>();

  const lines = text.split('\n');
  lines.forEach((line, li) => {
    if (!line.trim()) return;
    let o: Rec;
    try {
      o = JSON.parse(line) as Rec;
    } catch {
      badLines++;
      return;
    }
    if (o.type === 'summary' && typeof o.summary === 'string' && !title) title = o.summary;
    if (o.type !== 'user' && o.type !== 'assistant') return;
    const msg = o.message;
    if (!isRecord(msg)) return;
    sessionId ??= asString(o.sessionId) || undefined;
    cwd ??= asString(o.cwd) || undefined;
    gitBranch ??= asString(o.gitBranch) || undefined;
    const time = epochToIso(o.timestamp);
    if (time) {
      firstTime ??= time;
      lastTime = time;
    }
    if (o.isSidechain) sidechain++;
    const id = asString(o.uuid) || `l${li}`;
    const role = o.type === 'user' ? 'user' : 'assistant';
    const m = typeof msg.model === 'string' ? msg.model : undefined;
    if (m && !m.startsWith('<')) model = m;
    const isMeta = o.isMeta === true;

    if (typeof msg.content === 'string') {
      const cleaned = msg.content.replace(SYSTEM_REMINDER, () => {
        reminders++;
        return '';
      });
      if (!cleaned.trim()) return;
      const priv = isMeta || /^<(?:command-|local-command-)/.test(cleaned.trim());
      events.push({ id, kind: priv ? 'note' : 'message', role, time, text: cleaned.trim(), hidden: priv || undefined, meta: priv ? { private: isMeta } : o.isSidechain ? { sidechain: true } : undefined });
      return;
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(msg.content)) return;
    (msg.content as Rec[]).forEach((b, i) => {
      if (b.type === 'text' && typeof b.text === 'string') {
        const cleaned = b.text.replace(SYSTEM_REMINDER, () => {
          reminders++;
          return '';
        });
        if (!cleaned.trim()) return;
        b = { ...b, text: cleaned.trim() };
      }
      if (b.type === 'tool_use') toolNames.set(asString(b.id), asString(b.name));
      if (b.type === 'tool_result') b = { ...b, name: toolNames.get(asString(b.tool_use_id)) ?? 'tool', content: stripReminders(b.content, () => reminders++) };
      const before = events.length;
      pushBlock(events, b, `${id}-${i}`, role, time, m);
      if (isMeta) for (let k = before; k < events.length; k++) events[k] = { ...events[k], hidden: true, meta: { ...(events[k].meta ?? {}), private: true } };
      if (o.isSidechain) for (let k = before; k < events.length; k++) events[k] = { ...events[k], meta: { ...(events[k].meta ?? {}), sidechain: true } };
    });
  });

  if (!title) {
    const firstUser = events.find((e) => e.kind === 'message' && e.role === 'user' && e.text.trim().length > 3);
    title = firstUser ? firstUser.text.split('\n')[0].slice(0, 80) : 'Claude Code session';
  }

  const loss: LossItem[] = [];
  const encrypted = events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'reasoning' && e.redactedBySource).length;
  if (encrypted) loss.push({ category: 'reasoning-depth', count: encrypted, severity: 'info', detail: 'Thinking blocks are stored as signatures only (no plaintext) in this log, so reasoning cannot be transferred. Decisions are captured in the visible messages and tool calls.' });
  if (reminders) loss.push({ category: 'harness-context', count: reminders, severity: 'info', detail: 'Harness-injected <system-reminder> blocks were removed (private runtime context, not part of the work).' });
  if (badLines) loss.push({ category: 'unreadable-lines', count: badLines, severity: 'warn', detail: 'Some JSONL lines could not be parsed and were skipped.' });
  if (sidechain) loss.push({ category: 'subagents', count: sidechain, severity: 'info', detail: 'Sub-agent (sidechain) traffic is included and tagged; it may duplicate summaries already in the main thread.' });

  return {
    title,
    source: { kind: 'claude-code', tool: 'Claude Code', conversationId: sessionId, createdAt: firstTime, updatedAt: lastTime, model, cwd, gitBranch },
    events,
    loss,
  };
}

function stripReminders(content: unknown, onHit: () => void): unknown {
  if (typeof content === 'string') return content.replace(SYSTEM_REMINDER, () => (onHit(), ''));
  if (Array.isArray(content)) return content.map((c) => (isRecord(c) && typeof c.text === 'string' ? { ...c, text: c.text.replace(SYSTEM_REMINDER, () => (onHit(), '')) } : c));
  return content;
}
recovered · src/parsers/detect.ts · 3.2 KB
// Detect the format of an input and dispatch to the right parser.

import type { ParsedConversation, SourceKind } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { isRecord } from '../core/util.ts';
import { parseChatGPTExport, parseChatGPTShareHtml, parseChatGPTTurboStream } from './chatgpt.ts';
import { parseClaudeCodeJsonl, parseClaudeExport } from './claude.ts';
import { looksLikeMessages, parseMessagesLog } from './generic.ts';

export interface DetectResult {
  kind: SourceKind | 'baton-bundle';
  reason: string;
}

export function detectFormat(text: string, filename = ''): DetectResult {
  const t = text.trimStart();
  const name = filename.toLowerCase();
  if (t.startsWith('<') || name.endsWith('.html') || name.endsWith('.htm')) {
    if (t.includes('streamController.enqueue')) return { kind: 'chatgpt-share', reason: 'HTML with React Router stream (ChatGPT share page)' };
    return { kind: 'unknown', reason: 'HTML without a recognised conversation payload' };
  }
  if (name.endsWith('.jsonl') || isJsonl(t)) {
    if (/"type"\s*:\s*"(?:user|assistant|summary)"/.test(t.slice(0, 200000)) && /"sessionId"|"parentUuid"/.test(t.slice(0, 200000))) {
      return { kind: 'claude-code', reason: 'JSON Lines with Claude Code session records' };
    }
  }
  if (t.startsWith('[{"_') || /^\[\{"_\d+":/.test(t)) return { kind: 'chatgpt-share', reason: 'raw turbo-stream payload' };
  let json: unknown;
  try {
    json = JSON.parse(t);
  } catch {
    return { kind: 'unknown', reason: 'not JSON, JSONL or a recognised HTML page' };
  }
  const first = Array.isArray(json) ? json[0] : json;
  if (isRecord(first)) {
    if (first.schema === 'baton.bundle/v1') return { kind: 'baton-bundle', reason: 'already a Baton bundle' };
    if (isRecord(first.mapping)) return { kind: 'chatgpt-export', reason: 'conversation objects with a `mapping` tree (ChatGPT export)' };
    if (Array.isArray(first.chat_messages)) return { kind: 'claude-export', reason: 'conversation objects with `chat_messages` (Claude export)' };
  }
  if (looksLikeMessages(json)) return { kind: 'openai-messages', reason: '[{role, content}] message list' };
  return { kind: 'unknown', reason: 'JSON, but no known conversation shape' };
}

function isJsonl(t: string): boolean {
  const lines = t.split('\n', 3).filter(Boolean);
  if (lines.length < 2) return false;
  return lines.every((l) => l.trim().startsWith('{') && l.trim().endsWith('}'));
}

/** Parse any supported input into one or more conversations. */
export function parseAny(text: string, filename = '', url?: string): ParsedConversation[] {
  const d = detectFormat(text, filename);
  switch (d.kind) {
    case 'chatgpt-share':
      return [text.trimStart().startsWith('<') ? parseChatGPTShareHtml(text, url) : parseChatGPTTurboStream(text, url)];
    case 'chatgpt-export':
      return parseChatGPTExport(JSON.parse(text));
    case 'claude-export':
      return parseClaudeExport(JSON.parse(text));
    case 'claude-code':
      return [parseClaudeCodeJsonl(text)];
    case 'openai-messages':
      return [parseMessagesLog(JSON.parse(text), filename.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '') || undefined)];
    default:
      throw new Error(`Unsupported input${filename ? ` (${filename})` : ''}: ${d.reason}`);
  }
}
recovered · src/parsers/generic.ts · 2.2 KB
// Generic chat-completions style logs: [{role, content}] or {messages: [...]}.
// Covers OpenAI/Anthropic API request logs, LangChain dumps, Open WebUI exports, etc.

import type { BatonEvent, ParsedConversation } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { asString, isRecord } from '../core/util.ts';

type Rec = Record<string, unknown>;

export function looksLikeMessages(json: unknown): boolean {
  const list = isRecord(json) && Array.isArray(json.messages) ? json.messages : json;
  return Array.isArray(list) && list.length > 0 && list.every((m) => isRecord(m) && typeof m.role === 'string' && 'content' in m);
}

export function parseMessagesLog(json: unknown, title = 'Imported conversation'): ParsedConversation {
  const root = isRecord(json) ? json : {};
  const list = (Array.isArray(json) ? json : (root.messages as unknown[])) as Rec[];
  const events: Omit<BatonEvent, 'seq' | 'hash'>[] = [];
  list.forEach((m, i) => {
    const role = m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant' || m.role === 'tool' ? m.role : 'system';
    const id = `m${i + 1}`;
    if (Array.isArray(m.tool_calls)) {
      for (const tc of m.tool_calls as Rec[]) {
        const fn = isRecord(tc.function) ? tc.function : {};
        events.push({ id: `${id}-${asString(tc.id)}`, kind: 'tool_call', role: 'assistant', text: asString(fn.name), tool: { name: asString(fn.name) || 'tool', input: asString(fn.arguments), callId: asString(tc.id), language: 'json' } });
      }
    }
    const content = Array.isArray(m.content)
      ? (m.content as Rec[]).map((c) => (typeof c === 'string' ? c : asString(c.text ?? c))).join('\n')
      : asString(m.content);
    if (!content.trim()) return;
    if (role === 'tool') {
      events.push({ id, kind: 'tool_result', role: 'tool', text: content, tool: { name: asString(m.name) || 'tool', callId: asString(m.tool_call_id) || undefined } });
    } else {
      events.push({ id, kind: role === 'system' ? 'note' : 'message', role, text: content, hidden: role === 'system' || undefined, meta: role === 'system' ? { private: true } : undefined });
    }
  });
  return {
    title: asString(root.title) || title,
    source: { kind: 'openai-messages', tool: 'Chat log', model: asString(root.model) || undefined },
    events,
  };
}
recovered · src/pipeline.ts · 2.5 KB
// Browser-safe end-to-end pipeline: raw input text → parsed → bundle → file map.
// Used by the CLI, the MCP server and the web app.

import { buildBundle } from './core/build.ts';
import type { BatonBundle, BuildOptions, ParsedConversation } from './core/schema.ts';
import { slugify } from './core/util.ts';
import type { FileMap } from './core/zip.ts';
import { parseAny } from './parsers/detect.ts';
import { renderBundleFiles } from './render/files.ts';
import { buildSink, type SinkName, type SinkOptions } from './sinks/index.ts';

export interface ConvertOptions extends BuildOptions {
  url?: string;
  pick?: string; // conversation id or title substring, for multi-conversation exports
  sinks?: SinkName[];
  sinkOptions?: SinkOptions;
  keepSource?: boolean;
  skill?: boolean;
  html?: boolean;
}

export interface ConvertedBundle {
  slug: string;
  bundle: BatonBundle;
  files: FileMap;
}

export function pickConversations(list: ParsedConversation[], pick?: string): ParsedConversation[] {
  if (!pick) return list;
  const q = pick.toLowerCase();
  const hit = list.filter((c) => c.source.conversationId === pick || c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  if (!hit.length) throw new Error(`No conversation matches --pick "${pick}". Available: ${list.slice(0, 20).map((c) => `"${c.title}"`).join(', ')}${list.length > 20 ? ', …' : ''}`);
  return hit;
}

export function convert(text: string, filename: string, opts: ConvertOptions = {}): ConvertedBundle[] {
  const parsed = pickConversations(parseAny(text, filename, opts.url), opts.pick);
  const used = new Set<string>();
  return parsed.map((p) => {
    const { bundle, extraFiles } = buildBundle(p, opts);
    const extra: FileMap = { ...extraFiles };
    for (const s of opts.sinks ?? []) extra[`sinks/${s}.json`] = JSON.stringify(buildSink(s, bundle, opts.sinkOptions), null, 2) + '\n';
    if (opts.keepSource) extra[`source/${filename.replace(/^.*[\\/]/, '') || 'input.txt'}`] = text;
    let slug = slugify(bundle.title);
    if (used.has(slug)) slug = `${slug}-${bundle.id.slice(0, 6)}`;
    used.add(slug);
    return { slug, bundle, files: renderBundleFiles(bundle, { extraFiles: extra, skill: opts.skill, html: opts.html }) };
  });
}

/** Choose the right member of an account-export ZIP (conversations.json), given decoded members. */
export function pickExportMember(names: string[]): string | undefined {
  const pref = names.filter((n) => /(^|\/)conversations\.json$/i.test(n));
  if (pref.length) return pref[0];
  return names.find((n) => /\.jsonl?$/i.test(n)) ?? names.find((n) => /\.html?$/i.test(n));
}
stale · src/recover/artifacts.ts · 17.8 KB · rewritten by Python open(…, "w")
// Artifact recovery: rebuild the files an AI session created, from the code and tool calls it logged.
//
// Ops are extracted in event order and folded per path:
//   write/append  ← shell heredocs (`cat > f <<'EOF'`, `tee f <<EOF`), Claude Code Write
//   replace       ← Claude Code Edit / MultiEdit (applied exactly when the prior content is known)
//   touch         ← modifications we cannot replay statically (python open(p,'w'), sed -i, `> f`, cp/mv)
//   reference     ← files the conversation links to (sandbox:/mnt/data/...) but never shows the bytes of
// Result statuses: recovered (exact), stale (exact up to a later unreplayable change), lost.

import type { ArtifactStatus, BatonArtifact, BatonEvent, SourceKind } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { sha256Hex } from '../core/sha256.ts';
import { asString, isRecord } from '../core/util.ts';
import type { FileMap } from '../core/zip.ts';

export type FileOpKind = 'write' | 'append' | 'replace' | 'touch' | 'reference';

export interface FileOp {
  kind: FileOpKind;
  path: string; // absolute or original path as written in the source
  eventId: string;
  content?: string;
  old?: string;
  new?: string;
  all?: boolean;
  note?: string;
}

const IGNORED_PREFIXES = ['/tmp/', '/dev/', '/proc/', '/sys/', '/etc/', '/usr/', '/home/oai/', '/root/.'];
const IGNORED_PARTS = ['/node_modules/', '/.git/', '/__pycache__/'];

function ignorable(p: string): boolean {
  return IGNORED_PREFIXES.some((x) => p.startsWith(x)) || IGNORED_PARTS.some((x) => p.includes(x)) || p === '/dev/null';
}

function resolvePath(p: string, cwd: string): string {
  const unq = p.replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '');
  if (unq.startsWith('/')) return normaliseDots(unq);
  if (unq.startsWith('~')) return unq;
  return normaliseDots(`${cwd.replace(/\/$/, '')}/${unq}`);
}

function normaliseDots(p: string): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const seg of p.split('/')) {
    if (seg === '' || seg === '.') continue;
    if (seg === '..') out.pop();
    else out.push(seg);
  }
  return '/' + out.join('/');
}

// ───────────────────────────── shell scripts ─────────────────────────────

const HEREDOC_RE = /<<(-?)\s*(['"]?)([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\2/g;
const PATH_TOKEN = String.raw`(['"]?)([^\s'"<>|;&()]+)\1`;

/** Unwrap `bash -lc <script>` and JSON `{"cmd":["bash","-lc","..."]}` forms. */
export function unwrapShell(input: string): string {
  const t = input.trim();
  if (t.startsWith('{')) {
    try {
      const o = JSON.parse(t) as { cmd?: unknown };
      if (Array.isArray(o.cmd) && typeof o.cmd[o.cmd.length - 1] === 'string') return o.cmd[o.cmd.length - 1] as string;
    } catch {
      /* not JSON */
    }
  }
  return t.replace(/^(?:bash|sh)\s+-l?c\s+/, '');
}

function segmentAt(line: string, index: number): string {
  const seps = /&&|\|\||;|\|/g;
  let start = 0;
  let end = line.length;
  let m: RegExpExecArray | null;
  while ((m = seps.exec(line)) !== null) {
    if (m.index < index) start = m.index + m[0].length;
    else {
      end = m.index;
      break;
    }
  }
  return line.slice(start, end);
}

export function extractShellOps(input: string, eventId: string, initialCwd = '/'): FileOp[] {
  const script = unwrapShell(input);
  const lines = script.split('\n');
  const ops: FileOp[] = [];
  let cwd = initialCwd;
  const codeBodies: string[] = [];

  let i = 0;
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i];
    // `cd` changes the directory for the segments after it on the same line and for later lines.
    const lineCwd = cwd;
    const cds = [...line.matchAll(/(?:^|&&|;|\|\|)\s*cd\s+(['"]?)([^\s'";&|]+)\1/g)];
    const cwdAt = (idx: number): string => cds.reduce((c, m) => ((m.index ?? 0) < idx ? resolvePath(m[2], c) : c), lineCwd);

    const heredocs = [...line.matchAll(HEREDOC_RE)];
    // Modifications per command segment; heredoc segments (`cat > f <<'EOF'`) are writes, handled below.
    for (const seg of line.matchAll(/[^;&|]+/g)) {
      const start = seg.index ?? 0;
      const end = start + seg[0].length;
      if (heredocs.some((h) => (h.index ?? 0) >= start && (h.index ?? 0) < end)) continue;
      ops.push(...shellModifications(seg[0], eventId, cwdAt(start)));
    }
    cwd = cwdAt(line.length);

    let j = i + 1;
    for (const h of heredocs) {
      const strip = h[1] === '-';
      const quoted = h[2] !== '';
      const delim = h[3];
      const body: string[] = [];
      while (j < lines.length) {
        const raw = lines[j];
        const cmp = strip ? raw.replace(/^\t+/, '') : raw;
        j++;
        if (cmp === delim) break;
        body.push(strip ? cmp : raw);
      }
      const seg = segmentAt(line, h.index ?? 0);
      const catT = seg.match(new RegExp(String.raw`\bcat\b[^>]*?(>>?)\s*` + PATH_TOKEN));
      const teeT = seg.match(new RegExp(String.raw`\btee\s+(-a\s+)?` + PATH_TOKEN));
      const content = body.length ? body.join('\n') + '\n' : '';
      if (catT || teeT) {
        const append = catT ? catT[1] === '>>' : Boolean(teeT && teeT[1]);
        const path = resolvePath(catT ? catT[3] : (teeT as RegExpMatchArray)[3], cwdAt(h.index ?? 0));
        if (!ignorable(path)) {
          const expands = !quoted && /[$`\\]/.test(content);
          ops.push({ kind: append ? 'append' : 'write', path, eventId, content, note: expands ? 'unquoted heredoc: shell expansions ($VAR, `cmd`) were not evaluated' : undefined });
        }
      } else {
        // e.g. python - <<'PY' … PY: not a file, but its code may rewrite files.
        ops.push(...pythonModifications(content, eventId, cwdAt(h.index ?? 0)));
      }
    }
    i = j;
  }
  return ops;
}

function shellModifications(line: string, eventId: string, cwd: string): FileOp[] {
  const ops: FileOp[] = [];
  const add = (p: string, note: string) => {
    if (!/[./]/.test(p)) return; // not path-like (e.g. `x>0` inside an inline expression)
    const path = resolvePath(p, cwd);
    if (!ignorable(path) && !/^-/.test(p)) ops.push({ kind: 'touch', path, eventId, note });
  };
  // Output redirection (not 2>, &>, heredocs already stripped).
  for (const m of line.matchAll(new RegExp(String.raw`(?<![0-9&<>])(>>?)\s*` + PATH_TOKEN, 'g'))) add(m[3], m[1] === '>>' ? 'appended by shell redirect' : 'overwritten by shell redirect');
  for (const m of line.matchAll(new RegExp(String.raw`\bsed\s+-i\S*\s+(?:'[^']*'|"[^"]*"|\S+)\s+` + PATH_TOKEN, 'g'))) add(m[2], 'edited in place by sed -i');
  for (const m of line.matchAll(new RegExp(String.raw`\b(?:cp|mv)\s+(?:-\S+\s+)*\S+\s+` + PATH_TOKEN, 'g'))) add(m[2], 'replaced by cp/mv');
  for (const m of line.matchAll(new RegExp(String.raw`\bzip\s+(?:-\S+\s+)*` + PATH_TOKEN, 'g'))) add(m[2], 'archive produced by zip');
  return ops;
}

function pythonModifications(code: string, eventId: string, cwd: string): FileOp[] {
  const ops: FileOp[] = [];
  const vars = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const m of code.matchAll(/\b([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*=\s*(?:Path\(\s*)?(['"])([^'"\n]+)\2/g)) {
    if (m[3].includes('/') || /\.\w{1,6}$/.test(m[3])) vars.set(m[1], m[3]);
  }
  const touch = (raw: string, note: string) => {
    const lit = raw.match(/^(['"])(.+)\1$/);
    const p = lit ? lit[2] : vars.get(raw);
    if (!p) return;
    const path = resolvePath(p, cwd);
    if (!ignorable(path)) ops.push({ kind: 'touch', path, eventId, note });
  };
  for (const m of code.matchAll(/open\(\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*|(['"])[^'"\n]+\2)\s*,\s*['"](?:w|a|wb|ab|w\+|a\+)['"]/g)) touch(m[1], 'rewritten by Python open(…, "w")');
  for (const m of code.matchAll(/\b([A-Za-z_]\w*)\.write_(?:text|bytes)\(/g)) touch(m[1], 'rewritten by Python write_text');
  for (const m of code.matchAll(/Path\(\s*((['"])[^'"\n]+\2)\s*\)\.write_(?:text|bytes)\(/g)) touch(m[1], 'rewritten by Python write_text');
  for (const m of code.matchAll(/\.(?:save|savefig|to_csv|to_json|to_excel|write_pdf)\(\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*|(['"])[^'"\n]+\2)/g)) touch(m[1], 'generated by Python (binary or derived output)');
  return ops;
}

// ───────────────────────────── structured tools (Claude Code) ─────────────────────────────

export function extractToolArgOps(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, eventId: string): FileOp[] {
  const path = asString(args.file_path || args.notebook_path || args.path);
  if (!path) return [];
  if (name === 'Write') return [{ kind: 'write', path, eventId, content: asString(args.content) }];
  if (name === 'Edit') return [{ kind: 'replace', path, eventId, old: asString(args.old_string), new: asString(args.new_string), all: args.replace_all === true }];
  if (name === 'MultiEdit' && Array.isArray(args.edits)) {
    return (args.edits as unknown[]).filter(isRecord).map((e) => ({ kind: 'replace' as const, path, eventId, old: asString(e.old_string), new: asString(e.new_string), all: e.replace_all === true }));
  }
  return [{ kind: 'touch', path, eventId, note: `modified by ${name}` }];
}

// ───────────────────────────── text references ─────────────────────────────

export function extractSandboxReferences(text: string, eventId: string): FileOp[] {
  const ops: FileOp[] = [];
  for (const m of text.matchAll(/sandbox:(\/mnt\/data\/[^\s)\]"'>`]+)/g)) {
    const p = m[1].replace(/[.,;:]+$/, '');
    if (!p.endsWith('/')) ops.push({ kind: 'reference', path: decodeURI(p), eventId });
  }
  return ops;
}

// ───────────────────────────── fold ─────────────────────────────

export interface RecoveryResult {
  artifacts: BatonArtifact[];
  replay?: FileMap;
}

export function bundlePath(original: string, kind: SourceKind, cwd?: string): string {
  let p = original.replace(/^sandbox:/, '');
  if (p.startsWith('/mnt/data/')) p = p.slice('/mnt/data/'.length);
  else if (cwd && p.startsWith(cwd.replace(/\/$/, '') + '/')) p = p.slice(cwd.replace(/\/$/, '').length + 1);
  else if (p.startsWith('/')) p = '_abs' + p;
  // Never allow escaping the bundle directory.
  const safe = p.split('/').filter((s) => s && s !== '.' && s !== '..').join('/');
  return safe || (kind === 'unknown' ? 'file' : 'unnamed');
}

const BINARY_EXT = /\.(pdf|docx?|xlsx?|pptx?|zip|png|jpe?g|gif|webp|svg|mp4|mov|wav|mp3|pkl|npy|npz|parquet|sqlite|db)$/i;

export function collectOps(events: BatonEvent[], kind: SourceKind, cwd?: string): FileOp[] {
  const ops: FileOp[] = [];
  for (const e of events) {
    if (e.kind === 'tool_call' && e.tool) {
      const { name, input, args } = e.tool;
      if (args && isRecord(args)) ops.push(...extractToolArgOps(name, args, e.id));
      else if (input && (name === 'container.exec' || name === 'python' || name === 'Bash' || e.tool.language === 'bash')) {
        ops.push(...extractShellOps(input, e.id, kind === 'claude-code' && cwd ? cwd : '/'));
      }
    }
    if ((kind === 'chatgpt-share' || kind === 'chatgpt-export') && (e.kind === 'message' || e.kind === 'tool_call')) {
      ops.push(...extractSandboxReferences(e.text, e.id));
    }
  }
  return ops;
}

export function foldOps(ops: FileOp[], kind: SourceKind, cwd?: string): BatonArtifact[] {
  interface S { original: string; content?: string; versions: number; status: ArtifactStatus; from: string[]; modified: string[]; notes: Set<string> }
  const state = new Map<string, S>();
  const get = (p: string): S => {
    let s = state.get(p);
    if (!s) {
      s = { original: p, versions: 0, status: 'lost', from: [], modified: [], notes: new Set() };
      state.set(p, s);
    }
    return s;
  };

  for (const op of ops) {
    const s = get(op.path);
    switch (op.kind) {
      case 'write':
        s.content = op.content ?? '';
        s.versions++;
        s.status = 'recovered';
        s.from.push(op.eventId);
        s.modified = [];
        s.notes.clear();
        if (op.note) s.notes.add(op.note);
        break;
      case 'append':
        if (s.content !== undefined) {
          s.content += op.content ?? '';
          s.versions++;
          s.from.push(op.eventId);
        } else {
          s.content = op.content ?? '';
          s.status = 'stale';
          s.notes.add('appended to a file whose earlier content is not in the source');
          s.from.push(op.eventId);
        }
        break;
      case 'replace':
        if (s.content !== undefined && op.old !== undefined && s.content.includes(op.old)) {
          s.content = op.all ? s.content.split(op.old).join(op.new ?? '') : s.content.replace(op.old, () => op.new ?? '');
          s.versions++;
          s.from.push(op.eventId);
        } else {
          if (s.content === undefined) s.notes.add(kind === 'claude-code' ? 'pre-existing file edited during the session; only the edit hunks are in the transcript' : 'edit target content unknown');
          else s.notes.add('an edit did not match the reconstructed content');
          s.status = s.content === undefined ? 'lost' : 'stale';
          s.modified.push(op.eventId);
        }
        break;
      case 'touch':
        if (s.content !== undefined) s.status = 'stale';
        s.modified.push(op.eventId);
        if (op.note) s.notes.add(op.note);
        break;
      case 'reference':
        break;
    }
  }

  const artifacts: BatonArtifact[] = [];
  for (const [p, s] of state) {
    if (s.content === undefined) {
      if (kind === 'chatgpt-share' || kind === 'chatgpt-export') {
        s.notes.add(BINARY_EXT.test(p) ? 'binary/derived output; regenerate with replay/' : 'created by code the source did not include');
      } else {
        s.notes.add('exists in the original working directory; contents not reconstructable from the log');
      }
    }
    artifacts.push({
      path: bundlePath(p, kind, cwd),
      originalPath: s.original,
      status: s.content === undefined ? 'lost' : s.status,
      content: s.content,
      bytes: s.content === undefined ? undefined : new TextEncoder().encode(s.content).length,
      sha256: s.content === undefined ? undefined : sha256Hex(s.content),
      versions: s.versions || undefined,
      recoveredFrom: s.from.length ? s.from : undefined,
      modifiedBy: s.modified.length ? s.modified : undefined,
      note: s.notes.size ? [...s.notes].join('; ') : undefined,
    });
  }
  return artifacts.sort((a, b) => a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
}

export function recoverArtifacts(events: BatonEvent[], kind: SourceKind, cwd?: string): RecoveryResult {
  const artifacts = foldOps(collectOps(events, kind, cwd), kind, cwd);
  // De-duplicate bundle paths (two originals could map to the same safe path).
  const seen = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const a of artifacts) {
    const n = seen.get(a.path) ?? 0;
    seen.set(a.path, n + 1);
    if (n) a.path = a.path.replace(/(\.[^./]+)?$/, `~${n}$1`);
  }
  return { artifacts, replay: buildReplay(events) };
}

// ───────────────────────────── replay kit ─────────────────────────────

export function buildReplay(events: BatonEvent[]): FileMap | undefined {
  const cells = events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'tool_call' && e.tool?.name === 'container.exec' && e.tool.input);
  if (!cells.length) return undefined;
  const files: FileMap = {};
  cells.forEach((e, i) => {
    const n = String(i + 1).padStart(3, '0');
    files[`replay/cells/${n}.sh`] = `# Cell ${n} · source event ${e.id}${e.time ? ' · ' + e.time : ''}\n# ${e.tool?.title ?? ''}\n${unwrapShell(e.tool!.input!)}\n`;
  });
  files['replay/run-in-docker.sh'] = REPLAY_SH;
  files['replay/README.md'] = replayReadme(cells.length);
  return files;
}

const REPLAY_SH = `#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Re-run the code cells captured from the source conversation, in order, inside ONE throwaway container.
# The cells expect the ChatGPT sandbox layout, so the work dir is mounted at /mnt/data.
#
#   REVIEW cells/*.sh FIRST. They are third-party code captured from another AI's sandbox.
#
# Usage: ./run-in-docker.sh [workdir]
#   BATON_REPLAY_IMAGE    container image (default python:3.12-slim)
#   BATON_REPLAY_SETUP    shell run before the cells, e.g. "pip install numpy matplotlib python-docx"
#   BATON_REPLAY_NETWORK  docker network (default none; set to "bridge" if setup needs to download)
set -uo pipefail
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
WORK="$(mkdir -p "\${1:-$HERE/workdir}" && cd "\${1:-$HERE/workdir}" && pwd)"
IMAGE="\${BATON_REPLAY_IMAGE:-python:3.12-slim}"
NET="\${BATON_REPLAY_NETWORK:-none}"
SETUP="\${BATON_REPLAY_SETUP:-true}"
exec docker run --rm --network "$NET" -v "$WORK:/mnt/data" -v "$HERE/cells:/cells:ro" -w /mnt/data "$IMAGE" \\
  bash -c "$SETUP; for c in /cells/*.sh; do echo \\"── \\$(basename \\$c)\\"; bash \\"\\$c\\" || echo '   (cell failed, continuing)'; done"
`;

function replayReadme(n: number): string {
  return `# Replay kit

The source conversation ran **${n} code cell(s)** in its own sandbox. The source did not include their
outputs: tool results were redacted, and files could not be downloaded. These cells are the exact code that ran, in order.
Re-running them in a sandbox you control can regenerate derived outputs such as PDFs, figures and archives.

\`\`\`bash
# 1. Read every cell first. This is third-party code.
ls cells/ && less cells/*.sh
# 2. Run in an isolated container (no network by default)
BATON_REPLAY_NETWORK=bridge BATON_REPLAY_SETUP="pip install numpy matplotlib python-docx reportlab pypdf" ./run-in-docker.sh
# 3. Outputs land in ./workdir (mounted at /mnt/data inside the container)
\`\`\`

Known limits:
- Cells that call vendor-internal helpers (for example \`/home/oai/skills/...\`) will fail outside the vendor sandbox.
- Cells whose input the source blanked (for example ChatGPT \`functions.exec\`) are not here. Files they made are listed as **lost** in \`LOSS_AUDIT.md\`.
- Baton never runs these cells itself.
`;
}
recovered · src/render/files.ts · 1.8 KB
// Assemble the on-disk / in-zip layout of a bundle.
//
//   HANDOFF.md      start here (agents and humans)
//   PROMPT.md       one paste-ready block for chat apps
//   TRANSCRIPT.md   full chronological record
//   LOSS_AUDIT.md   what did not transfer, file statuses, integrity
//   index.html      offline viewer
//   bundle.json     canonical machine-readable record (baton.bundle/v1)
//   SKILL.md        makes the folder an Agent Skill ("continue this work")
//   AGENTS.md       pointer for Codex / Cursor / other agents
//   files/…         recovered artifacts
//   replay/…        re-run captured code cells in a sandbox (when available)
//   sinks/…         business-system payloads (optional)

import type { BatonBundle } from '../core/schema.ts';
import type { FileMap } from '../core/zip.ts';
import { renderAgentsMd, renderHandoff, renderPastePrompt, renderSkill } from './handoff.ts';
import { renderHtml } from './html.ts';
import { renderLossAudit, renderTranscript } from './markdown.ts';

export interface RenderOptions {
  skill?: boolean; // default true
  html?: boolean; // default true
  extraFiles?: FileMap; // replay kit, sinks, raw source…
}

export function renderBundleFiles(b: BatonBundle, opts: RenderOptions = {}): FileMap {
  const files: FileMap = {
    'HANDOFF.md': renderHandoff(b),
    'PROMPT.md': renderPastePrompt(b),
    'TRANSCRIPT.md': renderTranscript(b),
    'LOSS_AUDIT.md': renderLossAudit(b),
    'AGENTS.md': renderAgentsMd(b),
    'bundle.json': JSON.stringify(b, null, 2) + '\n',
  };
  if (opts.html !== false) files['index.html'] = renderHtml(b);
  if (opts.skill !== false) files['SKILL.md'] = renderSkill(b);
  for (const a of b.artifacts) if (a.content !== undefined) files[`files/${a.path}`] = a.content;
  Object.assign(files, opts.extraFiles ?? {});
  return files;
}

// HANDOFF.md (agent + human brief), PROMPT.md (one paste-ready block), SKILL.md, AGENTS.md.

import type { BatonBundle } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { slugify, truncate } from '../core/util.ts';
import {
  activityTitles,
  extractOpenThreads,
  fmtBytes,
  fmtDuration,
  fmtTime,
  lastVisibleAssistant,
  reasoningHeadlines,
  sourceLabel,
  splitTurns,
  toolUsage,
  userPrompts,
} from './turns.ts';

function quote(text: string): string {
  return text
    .trim()
    .split('\n')
    .map((l) => `> ${l}`)
    .join('\n');
}

export function renderHandoff(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const prompts = userPrompts(b);
  const first = prompts[0];
  const last = prompts[prompts.length - 1];
  const lastReply = lastVisibleAssistant(b)[0];
  const threads = extractOpenThreads(b);
  const tools = toolUsage(b);
  const s = b.stats;
  const a = s.artifacts;
  const high = b.loss.filter((l) => l.severity === 'high');
  const times = b.events.map((e) => e.time).filter(Boolean) as string[];
  const out: string[] = [];

  out.push('---');
  out.push(`baton: ${b.schema}`);
  out.push(`id: ${b.id}`);
  out.push(`title: ${JSON.stringify(b.title)}`);
  out.push(`source: ${JSON.stringify(sourceLabel(b))}`);
  if (b.source.url) out.push(`source_url: ${b.source.url}`);
  out.push(`root_hash: ${b.integrity.root}`);
  out.push(`generated: ${b.generator.generatedAt}`);
  out.push('---', '');
  out.push(`# Handoff: ${b.title}`, '');
  out.push(`> **If you are an AI agent picking this up:** this folder is an audited transfer of work done in ${b.source.tool}.`);
  out.push('> Read this file first and open `TRANSCRIPT.md` only for the turns you need. `files/` holds the files the source created,');
  out.push('> with the status of each listed in §6. **Do not assume anything in §7 exists.** It was lost in transit and must be rebuilt or re-run.');
  out.push('> Confirm the latest instruction (§3) with the user before large actions.', '');

  out.push('## At a glance', '');
  out.push('| | |', '|---|---|');
  out.push(`| Source | ${sourceLabel(b)}${b.source.url ? ` · <${b.source.url}>` : ''} |`);
  if (times.length) out.push(`| Span | ${fmtTime(times[0])} → ${fmtTime(times[times.length - 1])}${s.durationSeconds ? ` (${fmtDuration(s.durationSeconds)})` : ''} |`);
  out.push(`| Conversation | ${s.userMessages} prompts · ${s.assistantMessages} assistant messages · ${s.reasoningBlocks} reasoning blocks · ${s.toolCalls} tool calls |`);
  out.push(`| Files | ${a.recovered} recovered · ${a.stale} stale · ${a.lost} lost${a.attached ? ` · ${a.attached} attached` : ''} |`);
  out.push(`| Loss | ${high.length ? high.map((l) => `${l.count} ${l.category}`).join(' · ') : 'no high-severity loss'} |`);
  out.push(`| Integrity | \`${b.integrity.root.slice(0, 16)}…\` (run \`baton verify\`) |`, '');

  out.push('## 1. Original goal', '');
  out.push(first ? quote(first.text) : '_No user prompt found._', '');

  out.push('## 2. How the ask evolved (every prompt, verbatim)', '');
  prompts.forEach((p, i) => out.push(`${i + 1}. ${p.time ? `*${fmtTime(p.time)}*: ` : ''}${p.text.trim().replace(/\n+/g, ' ⏎ ')}`));
  out.push('');

  out.push('## 3. Where it left off', '');
  if (last) out.push('**Latest user instruction:**', '', quote(last.text), '');
  if (lastReply) out.push('**Last assistant response (excerpt):**', '', quote(truncate(lastReply.text, 1800)), '');

  out.push('## 4. Open threads and blockers', '');
  out.push('*Heuristically extracted from the final assistant messages. Verify before acting.*', '');
  if (threads.length) for (const t of threads) out.push(`- ${t}`);
  else out.push('- None detected.');
  out.push('');

  out.push('## 5. Reasoning trail (per turn)', '');
  for (const t of splitTurns(b.events)) {
    if (!t.prompt) continue;
    const heads = reasoningHeadlines(t);
    const calls = t.events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'tool_call').length;
    const head = truncate(t.prompt.text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(), 90);
    out.push(`- **Turn ${t.index}** (“${head}”): ${heads.length ? heads.join(' → ') : 'no reasoning summary'}${calls ? ` · ${calls} tool calls` : ''}`);
  }
  out.push('');

  out.push('## 6. Files', '');
  if (!b.artifacts.length) out.push('_None._');
  else {
    out.push('| Status | Path | Size | Notes |', '|---|---|---|---|');
    const rank = { recovered: 0, attached: 1, stale: 2, lost: 3 } as const;
    for (const f of [...b.artifacts].sort((x, y) => rank[x.status] - rank[y.status] || x.path.localeCompare(y.path))) {
      const link = f.content !== undefined ? `[\`${f.path}\`](files/${encodeURI(f.path)})` : `\`${f.path}\``;
      out.push(`| ${f.status} | ${link} | ${fmtBytes(f.bytes)} | ${(f.note ?? '').replace(/\|/g, '\\|')} |`);
    }
  }
  out.push('');

  out.push('## 7. What the source dropped', '');
  for (const l of b.loss.filter((x) => x.severity !== 'info')) out.push(`- **${l.severity.toUpperCase()} · ${l.category} (${l.count})**: ${l.detail}`);
  if (!b.loss.some((x) => x.severity !== 'info')) out.push('- Nothing significant.');
  out.push('- Full detail: `LOSS_AUDIT.md`.', '');

  if (tools.length) {
    out.push('## 8. Tool activity', '');
    out.push('| Tool | Calls | Inputs available | Outputs available |', '|---|---|---|---|');
    for (const u of tools) out.push(`| \`${u.name}\` | ${u.calls} | ${u.inputsAvailable}/${u.calls} | ${u.outputsAvailable}/${u.outputsTotal} |`);
    const titles = activityTitles(b, 25);
    if (titles.length) out.push('', '**What the tools were used for:** ' + titles.join(' · '));
    out.push('');
  }

  if (b.links.length) {
    out.push('## 9. References seen in the conversation', '');
    for (const u of b.links.slice(0, 40)) out.push(`- <${u}>`);
    if (b.links.length > 40) out.push(`- … ${b.links.length - 40} more in \`bundle.json\``);
    out.push('');
  }

  out.push('## 10. How to continue', '');
  const steps = ['Restate the goal and the latest instruction back to the user in two sentences, and ask whether anything changed.'];
  if (a.recovered + a.stale) steps.push(`Copy \`files/\` into the workspace as the starting tree.${a.stale ? ` Re-check the ${a.stale} stale file(s) against the transcript.` : ''}`);
  if (a.lost) steps.push(`Rebuild the ${a.lost} lost file(s) from their descriptions in \`TRANSCRIPT.md\`. Do not pretend they exist.`);
  if (b.events.some((e) => e.tool?.name === 'container.exec' && e.tool.input)) steps.push('Optionally regenerate derived outputs (PDFs, archives) with `replay/run-in-docker.sh` after reviewing the cells.');
  steps.push('Write `STATE.md` with your understanding (goal, decisions, next steps) so the next handoff starts from it.');
  steps.forEach((st, i) => out.push(`${i + 1}. ${st}`));
  out.push('', '---', `*Generated by Baton ${b.generator.version} · bundle id \`${b.id}\` · \`bundle.json\` is the canonical record.*`);
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

/** A single block a user can paste into any chat UI that cannot read folders. Budgeted to ~12k chars. */
export function renderPastePrompt(b: BatonBundle, budget = 12000): string {
  const prompts = userPrompts(b);
  const lastReply = lastVisibleAssistant(b)[0];
  const threads = extractOpenThreads(b, 6);
  const head: string[] = [];
  head.push(`I'm continuing a piece of work I started in ${b.source.tool}. Below is an exported handoff (made with Baton). Please read it, restate the goal and the current state in a few sentences, list what is missing, and then continue from the latest instruction.`, '');
  head.push(`TITLE: ${b.title}`);
  head.push(`SOURCE: ${sourceLabel(b)}${b.source.url ? ` (${b.source.url})` : ''}`, '');
  head.push('MY PROMPTS SO FAR (verbatim, in order):');
  prompts.forEach((p, i) => head.push(`${i + 1}. ${p.text.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}`));
  head.push('');
  const files = b.artifacts.map((a) => `- ${a.path} [${a.status}]`).join('\n');
  const loss = b.loss.filter((l) => l.severity !== 'info').map((l) => `- ${l.category} (${l.count}): ${l.detail}`).join('\n');
  const tail: string[] = [];
  if (threads.length) tail.push('OPEN THREADS / BLOCKERS:', ...threads.map((t) => `- ${t}`), '');
  if (files) tail.push('FILES FROM THE PREVIOUS SESSION (status):', files, '');
  if (loss) tail.push('WHAT DID NOT TRANSFER:', loss, '');
  tail.push('Please do not assume lost files exist. Ask me for anything you need.');

  const fixed = head.join('\n').length + tail.join('\n').length + 200;
  const room = Math.max(800, budget - fixed);
  const mid = lastReply ? ['WHERE THE ASSISTANT LEFT OFF (last reply):', truncate(lastReply.text.trim(), room), ''] : [];
  return [...head, ...mid, ...tail].join('\n') + '\n';
}

export function skillName(b: BatonBundle): string {
  return `continue-${slugify(b.title, 50)}`.slice(0, 64).replace(/-+$/, '');
}

export function renderSkill(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const first = userPrompts(b)[0]?.text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() ?? b.title;
  const desc = truncate(
    `Resume the "${b.title}" work handed off from ${b.source.tool}${b.source.createdAt ? ` (${b.source.createdAt.slice(0, 10)})` : ''}. Original goal: ${first}. Use when the user asks to continue, resume, rebuild, review or extend this work.`,
    1000,
  ).replace(/"/g, "'");
  const last = userPrompts(b).slice(-1)[0];
  return `---
name: ${skillName(b)}
description: "${desc}"
---

# Continue: ${b.title}

This skill folder is a **Baton handoff bundle**: an audited transfer of work done in ${b.source.tool}.
Everything needed to resume is next to this file.

## Steps

1. Read \`HANDOFF.md\` first. It has the goal, every user prompt, where the work stopped, open threads, files and losses.
2. Open \`TRANSCRIPT.md\` only for the turns you need. It is long, and reasoning and tool calls are folded.
3. Treat \`files/\` as the recovered working tree. Only **recovered** files are exact. **stale** files need checking.
   **lost** files do not exist and must be rebuilt (see \`LOSS_AUDIT.md\`).
4. Confirm the latest instruction with the user before large changes${last ? `:\n   > ${truncate(last.text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(), 300)}` : '.'}
5. When you finish a chunk of work, write \`STATE.md\` (goal, decisions, next steps) next to this file.

## Integrity

\`bundle.json\` is the canonical record. Hash-chain root: \`${b.integrity.root}\`.
If \`baton\` is installed, run \`baton verify .\` to check nothing was altered.
`;
}

export function renderAgentsMd(b: BatonBundle): string {
  return `# AGENTS.md: handoff context

This directory carries work transferred from ${b.source.tool} with Baton (\`${b.schema}\`).

- **Start with \`HANDOFF.md\`.** It has the goal, the verbatim prompts, the current state, open threads and the file status.
- \`TRANSCRIPT.md\` has the full chronological record, and \`bundle.json\` is the canonical machine-readable version.
- \`files/\` is the recovered working tree. Respect the status column (recovered / stale / lost).
- \`LOSS_AUDIT.md\` lists what the source did not include. Never assume a lost file exists.
- Ask the user to confirm the latest instruction before large changes.
`;

/** Wrap each "## Turn N" block of the transcript HTML in a <div class="turn"> for search filtering. */
function wrapTurns(html: string): string {
  const parts = html.split(/(?=<h2>(?:Turn \d+|Preamble)<\/h2>)/);
  return parts.map((p) => (/^<h2>(?:Turn \d+|Preamble)<\/h2>/.test(p) ? `<div class="turn">${p}</div>` : p)).join('\n');
}

export function renderHtml(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const s = b.stats;
  const files = b.artifacts
    .map((a) => {
      const head = `<span class="s-${a.status}">${a.status}</span> · <code>${escapeHtml(a.path)}</code> ${a.bytes !== undefined ? `· ${fmtBytes(a.bytes)}` : ''}${a.note ? ` · <span class="meta">${escapeHtml(a.note)}</span>` : ''}`;
      return a.content !== undefined ? `<details><summary>${head}</summary><pre><code>${escapeHtml(a.content)}</code></pre></details>` : `<p>${head}</p>`;
    })
    .join('\n');
  return `<!doctype html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<meta name="generator" content="Baton ${escapeHtml(b.generator.version)}">
<title>${escapeHtml(b.title)} · Baton handoff</title>
<style>${CSS}</style></head>
<body>
<header>
<div class="meta">Baton handoff · ${escapeHtml(sourceLabel(b))}</div>
<h1>${escapeHtml(b.title)}</h1>
${b.source.url ? `<div class="meta"><a href="${escapeHtml(b.source.url)}" rel="noopener noreferrer">${escapeHtml(b.source.url)}</a></div>` : ''}
<div class="chips">
<span class="chip"><b>${s.userMessages}</b> prompts</span>
<span class="chip"><b>${s.reasoningBlocks}</b> reasoning</span>
<span class="chip"><b>${s.toolCalls}</b> tool calls</span>
<span class="chip"><b class="s-recovered">${s.artifacts.recovered}</b> files recovered</span>
${s.artifacts.stale ? `<span class="chip"><b class="s-stale">${s.artifacts.stale}</b> stale</span>` : ''}
${s.artifacts.lost ? `<span class="chip"><b class="s-lost">${s.artifacts.lost}</b> lost</span>` : ''}
