recovered · src/render/markdown.ts · 5.2 KB
// TRANSCRIPT.md and LOSS_AUDIT.md

import type { BatonBundle, BatonEvent } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { fence } from '../core/util.ts';
import { fmtBytes, fmtDuration, fmtTime, sourceLabel, splitTurns } from './turns.ts';

const SEVERITY_LABEL = { high: 'HIGH', warn: 'WARN', info: 'info' } as const;

export function renderTranscript(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push(`# Transcript: ${b.title}`, '');
  out.push(`*${sourceLabel(b)}${b.source.url ? ` · <${b.source.url}>` : ''} · ${b.stats.userMessages} prompts · ${b.events.length} events · exported by Baton ${b.generator.version}*`, '');
  out.push('Reasoning, tool calls and tool outputs are folded into `<details>` blocks. Placeholders mark content the source did not include. See `LOSS_AUDIT.md`.', '');

  for (const t of splitTurns(b.events)) {
    out.push('---', '');
    if (t.prompt) {
      out.push(`## Turn ${t.index}`, '');
      out.push(`### User${t.prompt.time ? ` · ${fmtTime(t.prompt.time)}` : ''}`, '', t.prompt.text.trim(), '');
    } else {
      out.push('## Preamble', '');
    }
    for (const e of t.events) out.push(...renderEvent(e), '');
  }
  return out.join('\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n') + '\n';
}

function renderEvent(e: BatonEvent): string[] {
  const when = e.time ? ` · ${fmtTime(e.time)}` : '';
  switch (e.kind) {
    case 'message': {
      const label = e.role === 'user' ? 'User' : e.hidden ? 'Assistant (progress update)' : 'Assistant';
      return [`### ${label}${e.model ? ` · ${e.model}` : ''}${when}`, '', e.text.trim()];
    }
    case 'reasoning': {
      const body = e.redactedBySource && !e.text.trim() ? '*Reasoning was produced but the source stores it in encrypted form only.*' : e.text.trim();
      return ['<details><summary>Reasoning' + (e.meta?.granularity === 'summary' ? ' (summary)' : '') + '</summary>', '', body, '', '</details>'];
    }
    case 'tool_call': {
      const name = e.tool?.name ?? 'tool';
      const title = e.tool?.title ? ` · ${e.tool.title}` : e.text && e.text !== name ? ` · ${e.text}` : '';
      const lines = [`<details><summary>Tool call: <code>${name}</code>${escapeSummary(title)}</summary>`, ''];
      if (e.redactedBySource) lines.push('*Input not included by the source.*');
      else if (e.tool?.input) lines.push(fence(e.tool.input, e.tool.language ?? ''));
      lines.push('', '</details>');
      return lines;
    }
    case 'tool_result': {
      const name = e.tool?.name ?? 'tool';
      if (e.redactedBySource) return [`> Output of \`${name}\`: *redacted by source*`];
      const text = e.text.length > 20000 ? e.text.slice(0, 20000) + `\n… [${e.text.length - 20000} more characters in bundle.json]` : e.text;
      return [`<details><summary>Output: <code>${name}</code></summary>`, '', fence(text), '', '</details>'];
    }
    case 'attachment':
      return [`> Attachment: ${e.text}`];
    case 'note':
      return [`*${e.text.trim()}*`];
  }
}

function escapeSummary(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

export function renderLossAudit(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push(`# Loss audit: ${b.title}`, '');
  out.push('A transfer is only trustworthy if it says what it could **not** carry. This report lists everything the source dropped,');
  out.push('everything Baton reconstructed, and everything Baton removed on purpose (private context, secrets).', '');
  out.push('| Severity | Category | Count | Detail |', '|---|---|---|---|');
  for (const l of b.loss) out.push(`| ${SEVERITY_LABEL[l.severity]} | ${l.category} | ${l.count} | ${l.detail.replace(/\|/g, '\\|')} |`);
  out.push('');
  out.push('## Files', '');
  if (!b.artifacts.length) out.push('_No files were created or referenced._');
  else {
    out.push('| Status | Path | Size | Versions | Notes |', '|---|---|---|---|---|');
    for (const a of b.artifacts) out.push(`| ${a.status} | \`${a.path}\` | ${fmtBytes(a.bytes)} | ${a.versions ?? ''} | ${(a.note ?? '').replace(/\|/g, '\\|')} |`);
    out.push('');
    out.push('- **recovered**: reconstructed byte for byte from the code that wrote it. Its SHA-256 is in `bundle.json`.');
    out.push('- **stale**: exactly the last full write, but later code modified it in a way that cannot be replayed statically.');
    out.push('- **lost**: the conversation created or linked it, but its contents are not in the source.');
    out.push('- **attached**: shipped with the source export (e.g. uploaded file with extracted text).');
  }
  out.push('');
  out.push('## Integrity', '');
  out.push(`Hash chain root (SHA-256 over ${b.integrity.count} events + ${b.artifacts.length} artifact digests): \`${b.integrity.root}\``, '');
  out.push('Run `baton verify <bundle-dir>` to recompute it. Any edit to an event or a recovered file changes the root.', '');
  out.push('## Stats', '');
  const s = b.stats;
  out.push(`- Prompts: ${s.userMessages} · assistant messages: ${s.assistantMessages} · reasoning blocks: ${s.reasoningBlocks}`);
  out.push(`- Tool calls: ${s.toolCalls} · tool results: ${s.toolResults} · items the source redacted: ${s.redactedBySource}`);
  if (s.durationSeconds) out.push(`- Wall-clock span: ${fmtDuration(s.durationSeconds)}`);
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}
recovered · src/render/mdlite.ts · 4.6 KB
// A small, safe Markdown → HTML renderer for the transcript viewer.
// Every character of input is HTML-escaped first; only the constructs below are turned into tags.
// Supports: fenced code, headings, blockquotes, lists, tables, hr, paragraphs, **bold**, *em*,
// `code`, [links](http…), <autolinks>, <details>/<summary> produced by our own renderers.

import { escapeHtml } from '../core/util.ts';

function inline(s: string): string {
  let t = escapeHtml(s);
  const codes: string[] = [];
  t = t.replace(/`([^`\n]+)`/g, (_m, c: string) => {
    codes.push(`<code>${c}</code>`);
    return `\u0000${codes.length - 1}\u0000`;
  });
  t = t.replace(/\[([^\]\n]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+|[A-Za-z0-9_./%-]+)\)/g, (_m, label: string, href: string) => `<a href="${href}" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">${label}</a>`);
  t = t.replace(/&lt;(https?:\/\/[^\s&]+)&gt;/g, (_m, u: string) => `<a href="${u}" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">${u}</a>`);
  t = t.replace(/\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
  t = t.replace(/(^|[^*\w])\*([^*\n]+)\*(?!\w)/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
  t = t.replace(/\u0000(\d+)\u0000/g, (_m, i: string) => codes[Number(i)]);
  return t;
}

export function markdownToHtml(md: string): string {
  const lines = md.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n');
  const out: string[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  let para: string[] = [];
  const flush = () => {
    if (para.length) out.push(`<p>${para.map(inline).join('<br>')}</p>`);
    para = [];
  };

  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i];
    const fenceM = line.match(/^(`{3,}|~{3,})\s*([\w+-]*)\s*$/);
    if (fenceM) {
      flush();
      const close = fenceM[1];
      const body: string[] = [];
      i++;
      while (i < lines.length && lines[i].trim() !== close) body.push(lines[i++]);
      i++;
      out.push(`<pre><code${fenceM[2] ? ` class="lang-${escapeHtml(fenceM[2])}"` : ''}>${escapeHtml(body.join('\n'))}</code></pre>`);
      continue;
    }
    // Our own <details>/<summary> wrappers (renderers emit them on their own lines).
    const det = line.match(/^<details><summary>(.*)<\/summary>$/);
    if (det) {
      flush();
      const summary = det[1].replace(/<code>(.*?)<\/code>/g, (_m, c: string) => `\u0001${c}\u0002`);
      out.push(`<details><summary>${escapeHtml(summary).replace(/&amp;lt;/g, '&lt;').replace(/&amp;gt;/g, '&gt;').replace(/\u0001(.*?)\u0002/g, '<code>$1</code>')}</summary>`);
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (line.trim() === '</details>') {
      flush();
      out.push('</details>');
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (!line.trim()) {
      flush();
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    const h = line.match(/^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/);
    if (h) {
      flush();
      out.push(`<h${h[1].length}>${inline(h[2])}</h${h[1].length}>`);
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (/^(?:---|\*\*\*|___)\s*$/.test(line)) {
      flush();
      out.push('<hr>');
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (line.startsWith('>')) {
      flush();
      const body: string[] = [];
      while (i < lines.length && lines[i].startsWith('>')) body.push(lines[i++].replace(/^>\s?/, ''));
      out.push(`<blockquote>${markdownToHtml(body.join('\n'))}</blockquote>`);
      continue;
    }
    if (/^\s*\|.*\|\s*$/.test(line) && i + 1 < lines.length && /^\s*\|[\s:|-]+\|\s*$/.test(lines[i + 1])) {
      flush();
      const cells = (l: string) => l.trim().replace(/^\||\|$/g, '').split(/(?<!\\)\|/).map((c) => inline(c.trim().replace(/\\\|/g, '|')));
      const headCells = cells(line);
      i += 2;
      const rows: string[][] = [];
      while (i < lines.length && /^\s*\|.*\|\s*$/.test(lines[i])) rows.push(cells(lines[i++]));
      out.push(`<div class="tbl"><table><thead><tr>${headCells.map((c) => `<th>${c}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map((r) => `<tr>${r.map((c) => `<td>${c}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`);
      continue;
    }
    const li = line.match(/^(\s*)([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+(.*)$/);
    if (li) {
      flush();
      const ordered = /\d/.test(li[2]);
      const items: string[] = [];
      while (i < lines.length) {
        const m = lines[i].match(/^(\s*)([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+(.*)$/);
        if (m) {
          items.push(m[3]);
          i++;
        } else if (lines[i].trim() && /^\s{2,}/.test(lines[i]) && items.length) {
          items[items.length - 1] += ' ' + lines[i].trim();
          i++;
        } else break;
      }
      const tag = ordered ? 'ol' : 'ul';
      out.push(`<${tag}>${items.map((it) => `<li>${inline(it)}</li>`).join('')}</${tag}>`);
      continue;
    }
    para.push(line);
    i++;
  }
  flush();
  return out.join('\n');
}
recovered · src/render/turns.ts · 4.9 KB
// Shared analysis used by the renderers: turns, next steps, tool usage, timing.

import type { BatonBundle, BatonEvent } from '../core/schema.ts';

export interface Turn {
  index: number; // 1-based
  prompt?: BatonEvent; // the user message that opened the turn
  events: BatonEvent[];
}

export function splitTurns(events: BatonEvent[]): Turn[] {
  const turns: Turn[] = [];
  let cur: Turn = { index: 0, events: [] };
  for (const e of events) {
    if (e.kind === 'message' && e.role === 'user') {
      if (cur.events.length || cur.prompt) turns.push(cur);
      cur = { index: turns.length + 1, prompt: e, events: [] };
    } else {
      cur.events.push(e);
    }
  }
  if (cur.events.length || cur.prompt) turns.push(cur);
  // A preamble without a user prompt keeps index 0; renumber the rest from 1.
  let n = 0;
  for (const t of turns) if (t.prompt) t.index = ++n;
  return turns;
}

export function userPrompts(b: BatonBundle): BatonEvent[] {
  return b.events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'message' && e.role === 'user');
}

export function lastVisibleAssistant(b: BatonBundle, n = 1): BatonEvent[] {
  return b.events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'message' && e.role === 'assistant' && !e.hidden).slice(-n);
}

const NEXT_RE = /\b(next(?: step| milestone)?s?|not (?:yet|built|live|verified)|haven'?t|hasn'?t|still (?:need|to)|to ?do|remaining|need(?:s)? to|once you|after (?:you|that)|blocker|cannot|can'?t|couldn'?t|rejected|403|fail(?:ed|s)?|missing|pending|follow[- ]up|roadmap)\b/i;

/** Heuristic: sentences/bullets from the final assistant messages that describe open work or blockers. */
export function extractOpenThreads(b: BatonBundle, max = 10): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const e of lastVisibleAssistant(b, 2).reverse()) {
    const pieces = e.text
      .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ' ')
      .split(/\n+|(?<=[.!?])\s+(?=[A-Z*])/)
      .map((s) => s.replace(/^[\s>*#\-\d.)]+/, '').replace(/\*\*/g, '').trim())
      .filter((s) => s.length > 25 && s.length < 400);
    for (const p of pieces) {
      if (!NEXT_RE.test(p)) continue;
      const key = p.toLowerCase().slice(0, 80);
      if (seen.has(key)) continue;
      seen.add(key);
      out.push(p);
      if (out.length >= max) return out;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export interface ToolUsage {
  name: string;
  calls: number;
  inputsAvailable: number;
  outputsAvailable: number;
  outputsTotal: number;
}

export function toolUsage(b: BatonBundle): ToolUsage[] {
  const m = new Map<string, ToolUsage>();
  const get = (n: string) => {
    let u = m.get(n);
    if (!u) m.set(n, (u = { name: n, calls: 0, inputsAvailable: 0, outputsAvailable: 0, outputsTotal: 0 }));
    return u;
  };
  for (const e of b.events) {
    if (e.kind === 'tool_call' && e.tool) {
      const u = get(e.tool.name);
      u.calls++;
      if (!e.redactedBySource && (e.tool.input || e.tool.args)) u.inputsAvailable++;
    } else if (e.kind === 'tool_result' && e.tool) {
      const u = get(e.tool.name);
      u.outputsTotal++;
      if (!e.redactedBySource) u.outputsAvailable++;
    }
  }
  return [...m.values()].sort((a, b) => b.calls + b.outputsTotal - (a.calls + a.outputsTotal));
}

/** Labels such as "Searching OpenAlex…" that describe what tools were used for. */
export function activityTitles(b: BatonBundle, max = 40): string[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const e of b.events) {
    if (e.kind !== 'tool_call' || !e.tool?.title) continue;
    for (const t of e.tool.title.split(' · ')) {
      if (!t || seen.has(t)) continue;
      seen.add(t);
      out.push(t);
    }
  }
  return out.slice(0, max);
}

export function fmtTime(iso?: string): string {
  if (!iso) return '';
  return iso.replace('T', ' ').replace(/:\d\d(\.\d+)?Z$/, ' UTC');
}

export function fmtDuration(sec?: number): string {
  if (!sec || sec < 1) return '';
  const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  return h ? `${h}h ${m}m` : `${m}m ${sec % 60}s`;
}

export function fmtBytes(n?: number): string {
  if (n === undefined) return '';
  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`;
  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}

/** Bold lead phrases from reasoning summaries in a turn ("**Modeling cricket stumping.** …"). */
export function reasoningHeadlines(t: Turn, max = 6): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const e of t.events) {
    if (e.kind !== 'reasoning') continue;
    for (const m of e.text.matchAll(/\*\*([^*]{3,120}?)\.?\*\*/g)) {
      if (!out.includes(m[1])) out.push(m[1]);
    }
  }
  return out.slice(0, max);
}

export function sourceLabel(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const k = b.source.kind;
  const how = k === 'chatgpt-share' ? 'public share link' : k.endsWith('export') ? 'account data export' : k === 'claude-code' ? 'session log' : 'chat log';
  return `${b.source.tool}${b.source.model ? ` (${b.source.model})` : ''} · ${how}`;
}
recovered · src/sinks/index.ts · 10.0 KB
// Business-system sinks: turn a bundle into API-ready payloads for systems of record.
// Payload builders are pure (browser-safe). Posting lives in src/node/post.ts and is opt-in.

import type { BatonBundle } from '../core/schema.ts';
import { truncate } from '../core/util.ts';
import { renderHandoff } from '../render/handoff.ts';
import { extractOpenThreads, sourceLabel, userPrompts } from '../render/turns.ts';

export type SinkName = 'salesforce' | 'jira' | 'zendesk' | 'slack';
export const SINKS: SinkName[] = ['salesforce', 'jira', 'zendesk', 'slack'];

export interface SinkOptions {
  jiraProject?: string;
  jiraIssueType?: string;
  salesforceParentId?: string; // attach to an existing record (Opportunity, Account, Case…) instead of creating a Case
  salesforceApiVersion?: string;
}

export interface SinkPayload {
  sink: SinkName;
  method: 'POST';
  /** Path relative to the system's base URL (filled from env when posting). */
  path: string;
  body: unknown;
  notes: string[];
}

function summaryLine(b: BatonBundle): string {
  const s = b.stats;
  return `${s.userMessages} prompts · ${s.toolCalls} tool calls · files: ${s.artifacts.recovered} recovered / ${s.artifacts.stale} stale / ${s.artifacts.lost} lost`;
}

function goal(b: BatonBundle, n = 600): string {
  return truncate(userPrompts(b)[0]?.text.trim() ?? b.title, n);
}

function latest(b: BatonBundle, n = 600): string {
  const p = userPrompts(b);
  return truncate(p[p.length - 1]?.text.trim() ?? '', n);
}

function priority(b: BatonBundle): 'High' | 'Medium' | 'Low' {
  if (b.loss.some((l) => l.severity === 'high' && (l.category === 'files-lost' || l.category === 'tool-inputs'))) return 'High';
  if (b.loss.some((l) => l.severity !== 'info')) return 'Medium';
  return 'Low';
}

const toBase64 = (s: string): string => {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(s);
  let bin = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) bin += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
  return btoa(bin);
};

// ───────────────────────────── Salesforce ─────────────────────────────

export function salesforcePayload(b: BatonBundle, o: SinkOptions = {}): SinkPayload {
  const v = o.salesforceApiVersion ?? 'v61.0';
  const threads = extractOpenThreads(b, 5);
  const noteHtml = renderHandoff(b)
    .replace(/^---[\s\S]*?---\n/, '')
    .split('\n')
    .map((l) => `<p>${l.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')}</p>`)
    .join('');
  const parentRef = o.salesforceParentId ?? '@{case.id}';
  const req: unknown[] = [];
  if (!o.salesforceParentId) {
    req.push({
      method: 'POST',
      url: `/services/data/${v}/sobjects/Case`,
      referenceId: 'case',
      body: {
        Subject: truncate(`AI handoff: ${b.title}`, 255),
        Description: truncate(`${goal(b, 1500)}\n\nLatest instruction: ${latest(b, 800)}\n\n${summaryLine(b)}\nSource: ${sourceLabel(b)}${b.source.url ? ` ${b.source.url}` : ''}\nBaton bundle ${b.id} · root ${b.integrity.root}`, 32000),
        Origin: 'Web',
        Priority: priority(b),
        Status: 'New',
      },
    });
  }
  req.push({
    method: 'POST',
    url: `/services/data/${v}/sobjects/ContentNote`,
    referenceId: 'note',
    body: { Title: truncate(`Baton handoff · ${b.title}`, 255), Content: toBase64(noteHtml) },
  });
  req.push({
    method: 'POST',
    url: `/services/data/${v}/sobjects/ContentDocumentLink`,
    referenceId: 'link',
    body: { ContentDocumentId: '@{note.id}', LinkedEntityId: parentRef, ShareType: 'V' },
  });
  threads.forEach((t, i) =>
    req.push({
      method: 'POST',
      url: `/services/data/${v}/sobjects/Task`,
      referenceId: `task${i + 1}`,
      body: { Subject: truncate(t, 255), WhatId: parentRef, Status: 'Not Started', Priority: 'Normal', Description: `Open thread from Baton handoff ${b.id}` },
    }),
  );
  return {
    sink: 'salesforce',
    method: 'POST',
    path: `/services/data/${v}/composite`,
    body: { allOrNone: true, compositeRequest: req },
    notes: [
      o.salesforceParentId ? `Attaches to existing record ${o.salesforceParentId}.` : 'Creates a Case, attaches the handoff as a ContentNote, and adds one Task per open thread.',
      'Env for --post: SALESFORCE_INSTANCE_URL, SALESFORCE_ACCESS_TOKEN (OAuth access token from a Connected App).',
    ],
  };
}

// ───────────────────────────── Jira (Atlassian Document Format) ─────────────────────────────

type Adf = Record<string, unknown>;
const text = (t: string, marks?: string[]): Adf => ({ type: 'text', text: t, ...(marks ? { marks: marks.map((m) => ({ type: m })) } : {}) });
const para = (...content: Adf[]): Adf => ({ type: 'paragraph', content });
const heading = (level: number, t: string): Adf => ({ type: 'heading', attrs: { level }, content: [text(t)] });
const bullets = (items: string[]): Adf => ({ type: 'bulletList', content: items.map((i) => ({ type: 'listItem', content: [para(text(i))] })) });

export function jiraPayload(b: BatonBundle, o: SinkOptions = {}): SinkPayload {
  const prompts = userPrompts(b);
  const threads = extractOpenThreads(b, 8);
  const lostFiles = b.artifacts.filter((a) => a.status === 'lost').map((a) => a.path);
  const content: Adf[] = [
    para(text('Handed off from '), text(sourceLabel(b), ['strong']), text(` via Baton. ${summaryLine(b)}.`)),
    heading(3, 'Goal'),
    para(text(goal(b, 2000))),
    heading(3, 'Latest instruction'),
    para(text(latest(b, 1000) || '—')),
  ];
  if (prompts.length > 1) content.push(heading(3, 'Prompt history'), { type: 'orderedList', content: prompts.map((p) => ({ type: 'listItem', content: [para(text(truncate(p.text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(), 400)))] })) });
  if (threads.length) content.push(heading(3, 'Open threads / blockers'), bullets(threads));
  if (lostFiles.length) content.push(heading(3, 'Files to rebuild (lost in transfer)'), bullets(lostFiles.slice(0, 30)));
  content.push(heading(3, 'Integrity'), para(text(`bundle ${b.id} · sha256 root `), text(b.integrity.root, ['code'])));
  if (b.source.url) content.push(para({ type: 'text', text: 'Source conversation', marks: [{ type: 'link', attrs: { href: b.source.url } }] }));

  return {
    sink: 'jira',
    method: 'POST',
    path: '/rest/api/3/issue',
    body: {
      fields: {
        project: { key: o.jiraProject ?? '${JIRA_PROJECT}' },
        issuetype: { name: o.jiraIssueType ?? 'Task' },
        summary: truncate(`AI handoff: ${b.title}`, 250),
        labels: ['baton-handoff', `source-${b.source.kind}`],
        description: { type: 'doc', version: 1, content },
      },
    },
    notes: ['Env for --post: JIRA_BASE_URL, JIRA_EMAIL, JIRA_API_TOKEN, JIRA_PROJECT (or --jira-project).'],
  };
}

// ───────────────────────────── Zendesk ─────────────────────────────

export function zendeskPayload(b: BatonBundle): SinkPayload {
  const threads = extractOpenThreads(b, 8);
  const body = [
    `AI conversation handed off from ${sourceLabel(b)} via Baton.`,
    '',
    `Goal: ${goal(b, 1500)}`,
    '',
    `Latest instruction: ${latest(b, 800)}`,
    '',
    summaryLine(b),
    ...(threads.length ? ['', 'Open threads:', ...threads.map((t) => `- ${t}`)] : []),
    '',
    `Bundle ${b.id} · sha256 root ${b.integrity.root}`,
    b.source.url ? `Source: ${b.source.url}` : '',
  ].join('\n');
  return {
    sink: 'zendesk',
    method: 'POST',
    path: '/api/v2/tickets.json',
    body: {
      ticket: {
        subject: truncate(`AI handoff: ${b.title}`, 250),
        comment: { body, public: false },
        priority: priority(b).toLowerCase() === 'medium' ? 'normal' : priority(b).toLowerCase(),
        tags: ['baton_handoff', `source_${b.source.kind.replace(/-/g, '_')}`],
      },
    },
    notes: ['Creates a ticket with an internal note (public: false).', 'Env for --post: ZENDESK_SUBDOMAIN, ZENDESK_EMAIL, ZENDESK_API_TOKEN.'],
  };
}

// ───────────────────────────── Slack (Block Kit) ─────────────────────────────

export function slackPayload(b: BatonBundle): SinkPayload {
  const threads = extractOpenThreads(b, 5);
  const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  const blocks: unknown[] = [
    { type: 'header', text: { type: 'plain_text', text: truncate(`Handoff: ${b.title}`, 150) } },
    { type: 'context', elements: [{ type: 'mrkdwn', text: esc(`${sourceLabel(b)} · ${summaryLine(b)}`) }] },
    { type: 'section', text: { type: 'mrkdwn', text: `*Goal*\n${esc(goal(b, 700))}` } },
    { type: 'section', text: { type: 'mrkdwn', text: `*Latest instruction*\n${esc(latest(b, 500) || '—')}` } },
  ];
  if (threads.length) blocks.push({ type: 'section', text: { type: 'mrkdwn', text: `*Open threads*\n${threads.map((t) => `• ${esc(truncate(t, 280))}`).join('\n')}` } });
  const high = b.loss.filter((l) => l.severity === 'high');
  if (high.length) blocks.push({ type: 'context', elements: [{ type: 'mrkdwn', text: `:warning: ${esc(high.map((l) => `${l.count} ${l.category}`).join(' · '))}. See LOSS_AUDIT.md` }] });
  if (b.source.url) blocks.push({ type: 'actions', elements: [{ type: 'button', text: { type: 'plain_text', text: 'Open source conversation' }, url: b.source.url }] });
  return {
    sink: 'slack',
    method: 'POST',
    path: '(incoming webhook URL)',
    body: { text: `Handoff: ${b.title}`, blocks },
    notes: ['Env for --post: SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL (incoming webhook).'],
  };
}

export function buildSink(name: SinkName, b: BatonBundle, o: SinkOptions = {}): SinkPayload {
  switch (name) {
    case 'salesforce':
      return salesforcePayload(b, o);
    case 'jira':
      return jiraPayload(b, o);
    case 'zendesk':
      return zendeskPayload(b);
    case 'slack':
      return slackPayload(b);
  }
}

describe('sha256', () => {
  it('matches FIPS test vectors', () => {
    assert.equal(sha256Hex(''), 'e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855');
    assert.equal(sha256Hex('abc'), 'ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad');

describe('zip', () => {
  it('crc32 known value', () => {
    assert.equal(crc32(new TextEncoder().encode('123456789')), 0xcbf43926);
  });
  it('round-trips files with UTF-8 names', () => {
    const files = { 'a.txt': 'hello', 'dir/ü.md': '# hi\n', 'bin.dat': new Uint8Array([0, 1, 2, 255]) };
    const out = unzipStored(zip(files));
    assert.equal(new TextDecoder().decode(out['a.txt']), 'hello');
    assert.equal(new TextDecoder().decode(out['dir/ü.md']), '# hi\n');
    assert.deepEqual([...out['bin.dat']], [0, 1, 2, 255]);
  });
  it('reads deflated members via the central directory', () => {
    // Build a zip with one deflated member by hand (method 8).
    const data = new TextEncoder().encode('[{"title":"x","mapping":{}}]'.repeat(20));
    const comp = new Uint8Array(deflateRawSync(data));
    const stored = zip({ 'conversations.json': comp });
    // Patch method + uncompressed size in local and central headers.
    const v = new DataView(stored.buffer);
    v.setUint16(8, 8, true);
    v.setUint32(22, data.length, true);
    const cd = stored.length - 22 - (46 + 'conversations.json'.length);
    v.setUint16(cd + 10, 8, true);
    v.setUint32(cd + 24, data.length, true);
    assert.equal(listZip(stored)[0].method, 8);
    const out = unzipSync(stored, (d) => new Uint8Array(inflateRawSync(d)));
    assert.equal(new TextDecoder().decode(out['conversations.json']), new TextDecoder().decode(data));
  });
});

      assert.equal(r.apply(s), s, s);
    }
  });
  it('can be disabled', () => assert.equal(new Redactor(false).apply('[REDACTED:EMAIL]'), '[REDACTED:EMAIL]'));

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { gunzipSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { buildBundle } from '../src/core/build.ts';
import { verifyBundle } from '../src/core/integrity.ts';
import { sha256Hex } from '../src/core/sha256.ts';

const FIXTURE = new URL('../fixtures/chatgpt-share-cricket-stumping.html.gz', import.meta.url);

const NOW = { now: '2026-10-11T00:00:00Z' };

describe('ChatGPT share page (real fixture)', () => {

    assert.equal(bundle.source.conversationId, '6acbc222-2500-83ee-b38c-dcfe2b46e388');

    assert.ok(execs.every((e) => e.tool?.input && !e.redactedBySource));

    const by = Object.fromEntries(bundle.artifacts.map((a) => [a.path, a]));

    assert.equal(bundle.stats.artifacts.stale, 3);

    assert.equal(sim.status, 'recovered');
    assert.match(sim.content ?? '', /^#!\/usr\/bin\/env python3\n"""Reproducible illustrative model of cricket stumping/);
    assert.equal(sim.sha256, sha256Hex(sim.content ?? ''));
    assert.equal(by['stumping_research_package/README.md'].versions, 2);
    assert.equal(by['stumping_research_package/make_paper.py'].status, 'stale');
    assert.equal(by['stumping_research_package/app.py'].status, 'lost');
    assert.equal(by['stumping_research_package/stumping_recovery_research_paper.pdf'].status, 'lost');
  });

    assert.match(String(extraFiles['replay/run-in-docker.sh']), /--network "\$NET"/);
  });
  it('excludes empty private context and converts GenUI markers', () => {
    assert.ok(!bundle.events.some((e) => e.role === 'system'));
    const all = bundle.events.map((e) => e.text).join('\n');
    assert.ok(!/[-]/.test(all), 'private-use markers removed');
    assert.match(all, /\[github\.com\/nimb-ou\/App\]\(https:\/\/github\.com\/nimb-ou\/App\)/);
  });
  it('is deterministic and verifiable', () => {
    const again = buildBundle(parseAny(shareHtml, 'share.html', 'https://chatgpt.com/share/6acbc222-2500-83ee-b38c-dcfe2b46e388')[0], NOW).bundle;
    assert.equal(again.integrity.root, bundle.integrity.root);
    assert.ok(verifyBundle(bundle).ok);
    const tampered = structuredClone(bundle);
    tampered.events[0].text += ' (edited)';
    const r = verifyBundle(tampered);
    assert.equal(r.ok, false);
    assert.equal(r.firstBadEvent, tampered.events[0].id);
  });
});

describe('ChatGPT export (conversations.json)', () => {

    current_node: 'c',
    mapping: {
      r: { id: 'r', parent: null, children: ['a'], message: null },
      a: { id: 'a', parent: 'r', children: ['b', 'x'], message: { id: 'a', author: { role: 'user' }, content: { content_type: 'text', parts: ['Plan a trip'] }, create_time: 1700000001 } },
      x: { id: 'x', parent: 'a', children: [], message: { id: 'x', author: { role: 'assistant' }, content: { content_type: 'text', parts: ['old branch'] } } },
      b: { id: 'b', parent: 'a', children: ['c'], message: { id: 'b', author: { role: 'assistant' }, recipient: 'python', content: { content_type: 'code', language: 'python', text: 'print(1)' } } },
      c: { id: 'c', parent: 'b', children: [], message: { id: 'c', author: { role: 'assistant' }, content: { content_type: 'text', parts: ['Here is the plan. Email me at [REDACTED:EMAIL]'] } } },
    },
  };
  it('follows the active branch, records the dropped branch and redacts', () => {
    const list = parseAny(JSON.stringify([conv]), 'conversations.json');
    assert.equal(list.length, 1);
    const { bundle } = buildBundle(list[0], NOW);
    assert.deepEqual(bundle.events.map((e) => e.kind), ['message', 'tool_call', 'message']);
    assert.ok(!bundle.events.some((e) => e.text.includes('old branch')));
    assert.ok(bundle.loss.some((l) => l.category === 'branches' && l.count >= 1));
    assert.match(bundle.events[2].text, /\[REDACTED:EMAIL\]/);
    assert.equal(bundle.redaction.counts.email, 1);
  });
});

describe('Claude.ai export', () => {
  const conv = [
    {
      uuid: 'u1',
      name: 'Pricing page',
      created_at: '2026-01-01T10:00:00Z',

    assert.equal(bundle.title, 'Pricing page');

describe('Claude Code JSONL', () => {
  const lines = [
    { type: 'user', sessionId: 's1', cwd: '/repo', gitBranch: 'main', uuid: 'u1', timestamp: '2026-10-11T10:00:00Z', message: { role: 'user', content: 'Add a greeting <system-reminder>secret harness context</system-reminder>' } },
    { type: 'assistant', sessionId: 's1', uuid: 'a1', timestamp: '2026-10-11T10:00:02Z', message: { model: 'claude-x', role: 'assistant', content: [{ type: 'thinking', thinking: '', signature: 'abc' }, { type: 'text', text: 'Creating the file.' }, { type: 'tool_use', id: 't1', name: 'Write', input: { file_path: '/repo/hello.ts', content: 'export const hi = "hello";\n' } }] } },
    { type: 'user', sessionId: 's1', uuid: 'u2', timestamp: '2026-10-11T10:00:03Z', message: { role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'tool_result', tool_use_id: 't1', content: 'File created' }] } },
    { type: 'assistant', sessionId: 's1', uuid: 'a2', timestamp: '2026-10-11T10:00:04Z', message: { model: 'claude-x', role: 'assistant', content: [{ type: 'tool_use', id: 't2', name: 'Edit', input: { file_path: '/repo/hello.ts', old_string: '"hello"', new_string: '"hello, baton"' } }, { type: 'tool_use', id: 't3', name: 'Bash', input: { command: 'npm test', description: 'Run tests' } }] } },
    { type: 'user', sessionId: 's1', uuid: 'u3', isMeta: true, timestamp: '2026-10-11T10:00:05Z', message: { role: 'user', content: 'internal caveat' } },
  ].map((o) => JSON.stringify(o));

  it('parses, strips harness context and replays file edits', () => {
    const text = lines.join('\n');
    assert.equal(detectFormat(text, 'x.jsonl').kind, 'claude-code');
    const { bundle } = buildBundle(parseAny(text, 'x.jsonl')[0], NOW);
    assert.equal(bundle.source.cwd, '/repo');
    assert.equal(bundle.source.model, 'claude-x');
    assert.equal(bundle.events[0].text, 'Add a greeting');
    assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(bundle).includes('secret harness context'));
    assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(bundle).includes('internal caveat'), 'isMeta dropped by default');
    const f = bundle.artifacts.find((a) => a.path === 'hello.ts');
    assert.equal(f?.status, 'recovered');
    assert.equal(f?.content, 'export const hi = "hello, baton";\n');
    const tr = bundle.events.find((e) => e.kind === 'tool_result');
    assert.equal(tr?.tool?.name, 'Write');
    assert.ok(bundle.loss.some((l) => l.category === 'reasoning-depth'));
    assert.ok(bundle.loss.some((l) => l.category === 'harness-context'));
  });
});

    assert.ok(bundle.loss.some((l) => l.category === 'private-context'));
  });
  it('rejects unknown input clearly', () => {
    assert.throws(() => parseAny('just some text', 'notes.txt'), /Unsupported input/);
  });
});

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import type { BatonEvent } from '../src/core/schema.ts';
import { bundlePath, extractShellOps, foldOps, recoverArtifacts, unwrapShell } from '../src/recover/artifacts.ts';

const call = (id: string, input: string, name = 'container.exec'): BatonEvent => ({ id, seq: 0, kind: 'tool_call', role: 'assistant', text: '', tool: { name, input, language: 'bash' } });

describe('shell heredoc extraction', () => {
  it('recovers quoted heredocs exactly, with trailing newline', () => {
    const ops = extractShellOps("bash -lc mkdir -p /mnt/data/p && cat > /mnt/data/p/a.py <<'PY'\nprint('$HOME')\nPY\necho done", 'e1');
    const w = ops.find((o) => o.kind === 'write');
    assert.equal(w?.path, '/mnt/data/p/a.py');
    assert.equal(w?.content, "print('$HOME')\n");
    assert.equal(w?.note, undefined);
  });
  it('flags unquoted heredocs that would expand', () => {
    const ops = extractShellOps('cat > /mnt/data/x.txt <<EOF\nhome=$HOME\nEOF', 'e1');
    assert.match(ops[0].note ?? '', /unquoted heredoc/);
  });
  it('handles append, tee, <<- tab stripping and multiple heredocs', () => {
    const script = "cat >> /mnt/data/log.txt <<'A'\none\nA\ntee /mnt/data/t.txt <<-'B'\n\tindented\n\tB\ncat <<'C' > /mnt/data/c.txt\nc\nC";
    const ops = extractShellOps(script, 'e1');
    assert.deepEqual(ops.map((o) => [o.kind, o.path, o.content]), [
      ['append', '/mnt/data/log.txt', 'one\n'],
      ['write', '/mnt/data/t.txt', 'indented\n'],
      ['write', '/mnt/data/c.txt', 'c\n'],
    ]);
  });
  it('tracks cd per command segment', () => {
    const ops = extractShellOps('cd /mnt/data/pkg && python run.py > results/out.txt && cd /mnt/data && zip -qr bundle.zip pkg', 'e1');
    assert.deepEqual(ops.map((o) => o.path).sort(), ['/mnt/data/bundle.zip', '/mnt/data/pkg/results/out.txt']);
  });
  it('detects python in-place rewrites as touches', () => {
    const ops = extractShellOps("python - <<'PY'\np='/mnt/data/pkg/m.py'\ns=open(p).read().replace('a','b')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nPY", 'e1');
    assert.deepEqual(ops.map((o) => [o.kind, o.path]), [['touch', '/mnt/data/pkg/m.py']]);
  });
  it('does not mistake .zip in filenames or inline > for commands', () => {
    const ops = extractShellOps('zip -qr /mnt/data/a.zip README.md app.zip.md && python -c "print(1>0)"', 'e1');
    assert.deepEqual(ops.map((o) => o.path), ['/mnt/data/a.zip']);
  });
  it('unwraps JSON cmd form', () => {
    assert.equal(unwrapShell('{"cmd":["bash","-lc","ls -la"]}'), 'ls -la');
  });
});

describe('fold + statuses', () => {
  it('recovered → stale after unreplayable modification; rewrite resets', () => {
    const events = [
      call('1', "cat > /mnt/data/a.py <<'X'\nv1\nX"),
      call('2', "python - <<'PY'\nopen('/mnt/data/a.py','w').write('v2')\nPY"),
    ];
    let arts = recoverArtifacts(events, 'chatgpt-share').artifacts;
    assert.equal(arts[0].status, 'stale');
    assert.equal(arts[0].content, 'v1\n');
    events.push(call('3', "cat > /mnt/data/a.py <<'X'\nv3\nX"));
    arts = recoverArtifacts(events, 'chatgpt-share').artifacts;
    assert.equal(arts[0].status, 'recovered');
    assert.equal(arts[0].versions, 2);
    assert.equal(arts[0].content, 'v3\n');
  });
  it('sandbox links without content are lost; binaries point to replay', () => {
    const msg: BatonEvent = { id: 'm', seq: 0, kind: 'message', role: 'assistant', text: '[PDF](sandbox:/mnt/data/out/paper.pdf) and [app](sandbox:/mnt/data/out/app.py)' };
    const arts = recoverArtifacts([msg], 'chatgpt-share').artifacts;
    assert.deepEqual(arts.map((a) => [a.path, a.status]), [['out/app.py', 'lost'], ['out/paper.pdf', 'lost']]);
    assert.match(arts[1].note ?? '', /replay/);
  });
  it('replays Claude Code Write + Edit + MultiEdit exactly', () => {
    const ev = (id: string, name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): BatonEvent => ({ id, seq: 0, kind: 'tool_call', role: 'assistant', text: '', tool: { name, args } });
    const arts = recoverArtifacts(
      [
        ev('1', 'Write', { file_path: '/repo/src/a.ts', content: 'const a = 1;\nconst b = 2;\n' }),
        ev('2', 'Edit', { file_path: '/repo/src/a.ts', old_string: 'const a = 1;', new_string: 'const a = 10;' }),
        ev('3', 'MultiEdit', { file_path: '/repo/src/a.ts', edits: [{ old_string: 'b = 2', new_string: 'b = 20' }] }),
        ev('4', 'Edit', { file_path: '/repo/README.md', old_string: 'x', new_string: 'y' }),
      ],
      'claude-code',
      '/repo',
    ).artifacts;
    const a = arts.find((x) => x.path === 'src/a.ts');
    assert.equal(a?.status, 'recovered');
    assert.equal(a?.content, 'const a = 10;\nconst b = 20;\n');
    assert.equal(a?.versions, 3);
    const readme = arts.find((x) => x.path === 'README.md');
    assert.equal(readme?.status, 'lost');
    assert.match(readme?.note ?? '', /pre-existing/);
  });
  it('bundle paths can never escape the bundle', () => {
    assert.equal(bundlePath('/mnt/data/../../etc/passwd', 'chatgpt-share'), 'etc/passwd');
    assert.equal(bundlePath('/etc/passwd', 'claude-code', '/repo'), '_abs/etc/passwd');
    assert.equal(bundlePath('/repo/../x', 'claude-code', '/repo'), '_abs/x');
  });
  it('folds ops in order with replace on known content', () => {
    const arts = foldOps(
      [
        { kind: 'write', path: '/r/f', eventId: '1', content: 'hello world' },
        { kind: 'replace', path: '/r/f', eventId: '2', old: 'world', new: 'baton' },
      ],
      'claude-code',
      '/r',
    );
    assert.equal(arts[0].content, 'hello baton');
  });
});

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { after, describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { gunzipSync } from 'node:zlib';
import { handleMessage } from '../src/mcp/server.ts';
import { writeFiles, isShareUrl } from '../src/node/io.ts';
import { convert } from '../src/pipeline.ts';
import { buildSink } from '../src/sinks/index.ts';

const ROOT = new URL('..', import.meta.url).pathname;
const FIXTURE = join(ROOT, 'fixtures/chatgpt-share-cricket-stumping.html.gz');
const tmp = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'baton-test-'));
after(() => rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true }));

const html = gunzipSync(readFileSync(FIXTURE)).toString('utf8');
const [cricket] = convert(html, 'share.html', { url: 'https://chatgpt.com/share/6acbc222-2500-83ee-b38c-dcfe2b46e388', now: '2026-10-11T00:00:00Z', sinks: ['jira', 'salesforce', 'zendesk', 'slack'] });

describe('bundle layout', () => {
  it('contains every surface file', () => {
    for (const f of ['HANDOFF.md', 'PROMPT.md', 'TRANSCRIPT.md', 'LOSS_AUDIT.md', 'AGENTS.md', 'SKILL.md', 'index.html', 'bundle.json', 'replay/run-in-docker.sh', 'sinks/jira.json']) {
      assert.ok(f in cricket.files, f);
    }
    assert.ok('files/stumping_research_package/paper.md' in cricket.files);
  });
  it('HANDOFF.md carries goal, prompts, state and loss', () => {
    const h = String(cricket.files['HANDOFF.md']);
    assert.match(h, /^---\nbaton: baton\.bundle\/v1/);
    assert.match(h, /## 1\. Original goal/);
    assert.match(h, /8\. \*[^*]+\*: It is connevted/);
    assert.match(h, /403 Resource not accessible by integration/);
    assert.match(h, /\| lost \| `stumping_research_package\/app\.py`/);
  });
  it('SKILL.md has valid Agent Skill front matter', () => {
    const s = String(cricket.files['SKILL.md']);
    const name = s.match(/^---\nname: ([a-z0-9-]+)\n/)?.[1];
    assert.equal(name, 'continue-cricket-stumping-simulation');
    assert.ok(name!.length <= 64);
    const desc = s.match(/\ndescription: "(.*)"\n---/)?.[1] ?? '';
    assert.ok(desc.length > 50 && desc.length <= 1024);
  });
  it('PROMPT.md stays within its paste budget', () => {
    assert.ok(String(cricket.files['PROMPT.md']).length < 14000);
  });
  it('index.html escapes conversation content', () => {
    const [b] = convert(JSON.stringify([{ role: 'user', content: 'hi <script>alert("x")</script>' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'ok' }]), 'x.json');
    const page = String(b.files['index.html']);
    assert.ok(!page.includes('<script>alert'));
    assert.match(page, /&lt;script&gt;alert/);
  });
});

describe('sinks', () => {
  const b = cricket.bundle;
  it('jira uses ADF and labels', () => {
    const p = buildSink('jira', b, { jiraProject: 'OPS' }) as { body: { fields: { project: { key: string }; description: { type: string; content: unknown[] }; labels: string[] } } };
    assert.equal(p.body.fields.project.key, 'OPS');
    assert.equal(p.body.fields.description.type, 'doc');
    assert.ok(p.body.fields.labels.includes('baton-handoff'));
  });
  it('salesforce composite links note and tasks to the case', () => {
    const p = buildSink('salesforce', b) as { path: string; body: { compositeRequest: { referenceId: string; body: Record<string, string> }[] } };
    assert.match(p.path, /\/composite$/);
    const refs = p.body.compositeRequest.map((r) => r.referenceId);
    assert.deepEqual(refs.slice(0, 3), ['case', 'note', 'link']);
    assert.equal(p.body.compositeRequest[2].body.LinkedEntityId, '@{case.id}');
    assert.equal(p.body.compositeRequest[0].body.Priority, 'High');
  });
  it('salesforce can attach to an existing record', () => {
    const p = buildSink('salesforce', b, { salesforceParentId: '006xx0000012345' }) as { body: { compositeRequest: { referenceId: string; body: Record<string, string> }[] } };
    assert.equal(p.body.compositeRequest[0].referenceId, 'note');
    assert.equal(p.body.compositeRequest[1].body.LinkedEntityId, '006xx0000012345');
  });
  it('zendesk is an internal note; slack has blocks', () => {
    const z = buildSink('zendesk', b) as { body: { ticket: { comment: { public: boolean } } } };
    assert.equal(z.body.ticket.comment.public, false);
    const s = buildSink('slack', b) as { body: { blocks: { type: string }[] } };
    assert.equal(s.body.blocks[0].type, 'header');
  });
});

describe('node io safety', () => {
  it('refuses to write outside the output directory', () => {
    assert.throws(() => writeFiles(join(tmp, 'out'), { '../evil.txt': 'x' }), /Refusing to write outside/);
    assert.ok(!existsSync(join(tmp, 'evil.txt')));
  });
  it('only fetches public ChatGPT share URLs', () => {
    assert.ok(isShareUrl('https://chatgpt.com/share/6acbc222-2500-83ee-b38c-dcfe2b46e388'));
    assert.ok(!isShareUrl('https://chatgpt.com/c/6acbc222'));
    assert.ok(!isShareUrl('http://chatgpt.com/share/abc'));
    assert.ok(!isShareUrl('https://evil.example/share/abc'));
  });
});

describe('MCP server', () => {
  it('initializes and lists tools', async () => {
    const init = (await handleMessage({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 1, method: 'initialize', params: { protocolVersion: '2025-03-26' } })) as { result: { protocolVersion: string; serverInfo: { name: string } } };
    assert.equal(init.result.protocolVersion, '2025-03-26');
    assert.equal(init.result.serverInfo.name, 'baton');
    const list = (await handleMessage({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 2, method: 'tools/list' })) as { result: { tools: { name: string; inputSchema: object }[] } };
    assert.deepEqual(list.result.tools.map((t) => t.name), ['baton_import', 'baton_session', 'baton_inspect', 'baton_read_turn', 'baton_verify', 'baton_sink']);
    assert.equal(await handleMessage({ jsonrpc: '2.0', method: 'notifications/initialized' }), undefined);
  });
  it('imports via tools/call and reports errors as tool results', async () => {
    const out = join(tmp, 'mcp-out');
    const res = (await handleMessage({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 3, method: 'tools/call', params: { name: 'baton_import', arguments: { source: FIXTURE, out_dir: out } } })) as { result: { content: { text: string }[] } };
    assert.match(res.result.content[0].text, /13 recovered · 3 stale · 16 lost/);
    assert.ok(existsSync(join(out, 'HANDOFF.md')));
    const bad = (await handleMessage({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 4, method: 'tools/call', params: { name: 'baton_import', arguments: { source: '/nope' } } })) as { result: { isError: boolean } };
    assert.equal(bad.result.isError, true);
  });
});

describe('CLI (end to end)', () => {
  const run = (...args: string[]) => spawnSync(process.execPath, [join(ROOT, 'src/bin.ts'), ...args], { encoding: 'utf8', cwd: tmp });
  it('imports, zips, verifies, and detects tampering', () => {
    const out = join(tmp, 'cli-out');
    const r = run('import', FIXTURE, '-o', out, '--zip', '--now', '2026-10-11T00:00:00Z');
    assert.equal(r.status, 0, r.stderr);
    assert.match(r.stdout, /13 recovered · 3 stale · 16 lost/);
    assert.equal(run('verify', out).status, 0);
    assert.equal(run('verify', `${out}.zip`).status, 0);
    writeFileSync(join(out, 'files/stumping_research_package/LICENSE'), 'changed');
    const v = run('verify', out);
    assert.equal(v.status, 2);
    assert.match(v.stdout, /altered files: stumping_research_package\/LICENSE/);
  });
  it('prints help and fails cleanly on bad input', () => {
    assert.match(run('help').stdout, /baton import <source>/);
    const bad = run('import', 'https://example.com/whatever');
    assert.equal(bad.status, 1);
    assert.match(bad.stderr, /Only public ChatGPT share links/);
  });
});

stale · web/app.ts · 10.8 KB · edited in place by sed -i
// Baton web app: client-side only. Parses the dropped/pasted conversation in the browser; nothing is uploaded.

import { listZip, zip } from '../src/core/zip.ts';
import { convert, pickExportMember, type ConvertedBundle } from '../src/pipeline.ts';
import { parseAny } from '../src/parsers/detect.ts';
import { markdownToHtml } from '../src/render/mdlite.ts';
import { fmtBytes } from '../src/render/turns.ts';
import { escapeHtml } from '../src/core/util.ts';

declare const __SAMPLE__: string;
declare const __SAMPLE_URL__: string;

type ClaudeHost = { use?: (name: string) => Promise<unknown> };
type Downloads = { save: (r: { filename: string; data: string | Blob | ArrayBufferView }) => Promise<{ status: string }> };

const $ = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string) => document.getElementById(id) as T;
const state: { text: string; filename: string; url?: string; current?: ConvertedBundle; tab: string; isSample: boolean } = { text: '', filename: '', tab: 'handoff', isSample: false };

// ───────── saving files (artifact viewer capability, or a plain download) ─────────

let downloads: Downloads | null | undefined;
const host = (window as unknown as { claude?: ClaudeHost }).claude;
if (host?.use) {
  host.use('downloads').then((d) => (downloads = d as Downloads | null), () => (downloads = null));
} else downloads = undefined; // standalone page: native downloads work

async function saveFile(filename: string, data: string | Uint8Array): Promise<void> {
  if (host?.use) {
    if (!downloads) return setStatus('Saving files is not available in this view. Use the copy buttons instead.', 'warn');
    try {
      await downloads.save({ filename, data });
      setStatus(`Saved ${filename}.`, 'ok');
    } catch (e) {
      const code = (e as { code?: string }).code;
      if (code !== 'declined') setStatus(`Could not save ${filename} (${code ?? 'error'}).`, 'warn');
    }
    return;
  }
  const blob = new Blob([data as BlobPart], { type: filename.endsWith('.zip') ? 'application/zip' : 'text/markdown' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = filename;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000);
}

async function copy(text: string, label: string, btn: HTMLElement): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    flash(btn, 'Copied');
    setStatus(`${label} copied. Paste it into ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini or any agent.`, 'ok');
  } catch {
    showTab('prompt');
    const ta = document.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('#panel textarea');
    ta?.focus();
    ta?.select();
    setStatus('Clipboard blocked here. The text is selected, so press Ctrl/Cmd+C.', 'warn');
  }
}

function flash(btn: HTMLElement, text: string) {
  const old = btn.textContent;
  btn.textContent = text;
  setTimeout(() => (btn.textContent = old), 1400);
}

function setStatus(msg: string, kind: 'ok' | 'warn' | 'err' | '' = '') {
  const el = $('status');
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.dataset.kind = kind;
}

// ───────── reading input ─────────

async function inflateRaw(data: Uint8Array): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const ds = new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw');
  const stream = new Blob([data as BlobPart]).stream().pipeThrough(ds);
  return new Uint8Array(await new Response(stream).arrayBuffer());
}

async function readFile(file: File): Promise<{ text: string; filename: string }> {
  const buf = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
  if (buf[0] === 0x50 && buf[1] === 0x4b) {
    const entries = listZip(buf).filter((e) => /\.(json|jsonl|html?)$/i.test(e.name));
    const name = pickExportMember(entries.map((e) => e.name));
    const entry = entries.find((e) => e.name === name);
    if (!entry) throw new Error('This zip has no conversations.json, .jsonl or .html inside.');
    const raw = entry.method === 8 ? await inflateRaw(entry.compressed) : entry.compressed;
    return { text: new TextDecoder().decode(raw), filename: entry.name };
  }
  return { text: new TextDecoder().decode(buf), filename: file.name };
}

async function load(text: string, filename: string, opts: { url?: string; sample?: boolean } = {}) {
  state.text = text;
  state.filename = filename;
  state.url = opts.url;
  state.isSample = !!opts.sample;
  const picker = $<HTMLSelectElement>('pick');
  const list = parseAny(text, filename, opts.url);
  if (list.length > 1) {
    picker.innerHTML = list.map((c, i) => `<option value="${i}">${escapeHtml(c.title)} · ${c.events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'message' && e.role === 'user').length} prompts</option>`).join('');
    $('pickerRow').hidden = false;
  } else $('pickerRow').hidden = true;
  run();
}

function run() {
  if (!state.text) return;
  const t0 = performance.now();
  try {
    const list = parseAny(state.text, state.filename, state.url);
    const idx = list.length > 1 ? Number($<HTMLSelectElement>('pick').value || 0) : 0;
    const pick = list.length > 1 ? list[idx].source.conversationId ?? list[idx].title : undefined;
    const [res] = convert(state.text, state.filename, {
      url: state.url,
      pick,
      redact: $<HTMLInputElement>('optRedact').checked,
      includeHidden: $<HTMLInputElement>('optHidden').checked,
      html: true,
    });
    state.current = res;
    render();
    const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
    setStatus(state.isSample ? `Example loaded: a real public ChatGPT share link, converted in ${ms} ms. Drop your own file to replace it.` : `Converted ${state.filename} in ${ms} ms. Nothing left this browser.`, 'ok');
  } catch (e) {
    setStatus(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e), 'err');
  }
}

// ───────── rendering ─────────

function render() {
  const r = state.current;
  if (!r) return;
  const b = r.bundle;
  const s = b.stats;
  const a = s.artifacts;
  const total = Math.max(1, a.recovered + a.stale + a.lost + a.attached);
  $('mTitle').textContent = b.title;
  $('mSource').textContent = `${b.source.tool}${b.source.model ? ` · ${b.source.model}` : ''} · ${b.source.kind}`;
  $('mSample').hidden = !state.isSample;
  $('mCounts').innerHTML = [
    ['prompts', s.userMessages],
    ['replies', s.assistantMessages],
    ['reasoning', s.reasoningBlocks],
    ['tool calls', s.toolCalls],
  ]
    .map(([k, v]) => `<div><dt>${k}</dt><dd>${v}</dd></div>`)
    .join('');
  $('mBar').innerHTML = (['recovered', 'attached', 'stale', 'lost'] as const)
    .filter((k) => a[k])
    .map((k) => `<span class="seg s-${k}" style="flex:${a[k] / total}" title="${a[k]} ${k}"></span>`)
    .join('') || '<span class="seg empty" style="flex:1"></span>';
  $('mLegend').innerHTML = (['recovered', 'attached', 'stale', 'lost'] as const)
    .filter((k) => a[k] || k !== 'attached')
    .map((k) => `<span><i class="dot s-${k}"></i>${a[k]} ${k}</span>`)
    .join('');
  const high = b.loss.filter((l) => l.severity === 'high');
  $('mLoss').innerHTML = high.length
    ? high.map((l) => `<li><b>${l.count}</b> ${escapeHtml(l.category.replace(/-/g, ' '))}</li>`).join('')
    : '<li class="none">No high-severity loss</li>';
  $('mRoot').textContent = b.integrity.root;
  $('out').hidden = false;
  showTab(state.tab);
}

function showTab(tab: string) {
  state.tab = tab;
  document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-tab]').forEach((t) => t.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(t.dataset.tab === tab)));
  const r = state.current;
  if (!r) return;
  const p = $('panel');
  const f = r.files;
  if (tab === 'handoff') p.innerHTML = `<div class="md">${markdownToHtml(String(f['HANDOFF.md']).replace(/^---[\s\S]*?---\n/, ''))}</div>`;
  else if (tab === 'prompt') p.innerHTML = `<p class="hint">One block for any chat app that cannot open folders. Paste it as your first message.</p><textarea readonly id="promptText" aria-label="Paste-ready prompt">${escapeHtml(String(f['PROMPT.md']))}</textarea>`;
  else if (tab === 'files') {
    const arts = r.bundle.artifacts;
    p.innerHTML = arts.length
      ? `<ul class="files">${arts
          .map((x) => `<li><span class="tag s-${x.status}">${x.status}</span><code>${escapeHtml(x.path)}</code><span class="sz">${fmtBytes(x.bytes)}</span>${x.note ? `<small>${escapeHtml(x.note)}</small>` : ''}</li>`)
          .join('')}</ul>`
      : '<p class="hint">This conversation did not create or reference files.</p>';
  } else if (tab === 'loss') p.innerHTML = `<div class="md">${markdownToHtml(String(f['LOSS_AUDIT.md']))}</div>`;
}

// ───────── wiring ─────────

function wire() {
  const drop = $('drop');
  const file = $<HTMLInputElement>('file');
  const take = async (fl: FileList | null) => {
    const f = fl?.[0];
    if (!f) return;
    setStatus(`Reading ${f.name}…`);
    try {
      const { text, filename } = await readFile(f);
      await load(text, filename);
    } catch (e) {
      setStatus(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e), 'err');
    }
  };
  file.addEventListener('change', () => take(file.files));
  drop.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    drop.classList.add('over');
  });
  drop.addEventListener('dragleave', () => drop.classList.remove('over'));
  drop.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    drop.classList.remove('over');
    take(e.dataTransfer?.files ?? null);
  });
  $('pasteGo').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const t = $<HTMLTextAreaElement>('paste').value;
    if (!t.trim()) return setStatus('Paste a share page, export JSON or session log first.', 'warn');
    load(t, 'pasted.txt').catch((e) => setStatus(String(e instanceof Error ? e.message : e), 'err'));
  });
  $('optRedact').addEventListener('change', run);
  $('optHidden').addEventListener('change', run);
  $('pick').addEventListener('change', run);
  document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-tab]').forEach((t) => t.addEventListener('click', () => showTab(t.dataset.tab!)));

  $('bCopyPrompt').addEventListener('click', (e) => state.current && copy(String(state.current.files['PROMPT.md']), 'Paste-ready prompt', e.currentTarget as HTMLElement));
  $('bCopyHandoff').addEventListener('click', (e) => state.current && copy(String(state.current.files['HANDOFF.md']), 'HANDOFF.md', e.currentTarget as HTMLElement));
  $('bZip').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const r = state.current;
    if (!r) return;
    const prefixed: Record<string, string | Uint8Array> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(r.files)) prefixed[`${r.slug}/${k}`] = v;
    saveFile(`${r.slug}.baton.zip`, zip(prefixed));
  });
  $('bHandoff').addEventListener('click', () => state.current && saveFile(`${state.current.slug}.HANDOFF.md`, String(state.current.files['HANDOFF.md'])));
  $('bCli').addEventListener('click', (e) => copy($('cliCmd').textContent ?? '', 'CLI command', e.currentTarget as HTMLElement));
}

wire();
if (typeof __SAMPLE__ === 'string' && __SAMPLE__) {
  load(__SAMPLE__, 'share.html', { url: __SAMPLE_URL__ || undefined, sample: true }).catch((e) => setStatus(String(e), 'err'));
}
